We glean additional interesting insights, including (a) the importance of evaluating strategies beyond the main line, (b) the limitations of pure concept-based descriptions, and (c) the limitations of relying on LLMs rather than humans for evaluation.

</details>


### [75] [LightMem-Ego: Your AI Memory for Everyday Life](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11487)
*Yijun Chen,Boyi Xiao,Yixian Zhao,Haoting Xia,Buqiang Xu,Jizhan Fang,Yanya Li,Yaqi Zheng,Xuehai Wang,Zirui Xue,Liuxin Zhang,Hui Li,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LightMem-Ego 是一个轻量级流式多模态记忆系统，可部署在手机和AI眼镜上，通过持续捕获用户的第一人称视觉和音频流，构建分层记忆结构（当前、短期、长期），以回答关于过去经历的查询，支持物品查找、对话回忆、生活总结等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的个人AI助手缺乏轻量级的多模态记忆系统来持续积累、组织和检索用户长期的生活经历，难以回答关于过去经历的查询。

Method: 系统持续捕获第一人称视觉和音频流，在共享时间线上对齐，组织成包含当前、短期和长期记忆的分层结构；根据用户查询动态路由到合适的记忆层级，生成基于多模态证据的答案。

Result: 该系统可在智能手机和AI眼镜上部署，实现了物品查找、对话回忆、生活总结、日常规律发现和个性化辅助等功能。

Conclusion: LightMem-Ego 为轻量级流式多模态记忆提供了一种可行方案，能在移动和可穿戴设备上有效支持日常生活辅助任务。

Abstract: Personal AI assistants on mobile and wearable devices continuously perceive users' daily lives through visual and audio streams. However, answering queries about past experiences requires lightweight multimodal memory that can continuously accumulate, organize, and retrieve long-term experiences, which remains challenging. To address this challenge, we present LightMem-Ego, a lightweight streaming multimodal memory system for everyday-life assistance. The system continuously captures egocentric visual and audio streams, aligns them on a shared timeline, and organizes them into a hierarchical memory consisting of current, short-term, and long-term memory. Given a user query, LightMem-Ego dynamically routes retrieval to the appropriate memory level and generates answers grounded in multimodal evidence. The demonstration can be deployed on smartphones and AI glasses, supporting object finding, conversation recall, life summarization, routine discovery, and personalized assistance. Code is available at https://github.com/zjunlp/LightMem-Ego.

</details>


### [76] [GEIS: A Generation-Evaluation-Improvement Loop of Agent Skills for Long-Form Article Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11503)
*Jiale Zhang,Juntao Hu,Zhijian Ou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GEIS——一种基于命名化、声明式技能的评价驱动改进循环框架，用于维基百科风格的长文生成。在Tasi Harness中实现，通过写作、评估、改进等模块化技能，在20个维基百科特色话题上相比基线提升8分，并将写作技能平均分从82.90提升至86.95。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文生成的多智能体流水线（如STORM）将能力纠缠在提示词和固定流程中，难以检查、复用或迭代改进。本文旨在将长文生成从固定工作流重构为可检查、模块化且评价引导的改进循环。

Method: GEIS框架包含三大核心技能：写作技能（Request→Plan→Draft→Audit→Refine→Deliver）、成对评估技能（基于PDF的结构化质量报告）和改进技能（将评估中反复出现的发现映射为永久性补丁写入写作技能）。所有技能均为命名化、声明式设计，在Tasi Harness中实现。

Result: 在20个维基百科特色话题上，GEIS相比Tasi Harness默认写作器在PDF质量评分上提升8分（100分量表），在两个可比维度（结构质量和内容质量）上超越STORM。改进实验中，打补丁后的写作技能平均分从82.90提升至86.95，20个话题中17个得到改进，增益主要来自内容质量。

Conclusion: 长文生成可以被重构为可检查、模块化、评价驱动的改进循环，而非固定的工作流，这一范式在质量提升和可迭代性上均表现出显著优势。

Abstract: Long-form article generation remains difficult for large language models because it combines long context, long instructions, and long outputs. Existing multi-agent pipelines such as STORM improve information coverage by simulating role-specialized agents, but their capabilities are often entangled in prompts and fixed procedures, making them hard to inspect, reuse, or iteratively improve. This paper presents GEIS (Generation-Evaluation-Improvement loop of agent Skills), a loop of named and declarative skills for Wikipedia-style long-form article generation. Implemented and evaluated in Tasi Harness, GEIS composes skills for article writing, browser-based evidence and image collection, diagram rendering, PDF-aware pairwise evaluation, and rule-level skill improvement. Its core writing skill follows Request, Plan, Draft, Audit, Refine, and Deliver; the pairwise evaluation skill produces structured quality reports; and the improvement skill maps recurrent findings into permanent patches to the writing skill in our 20-topic experiment. We evaluate GEIS on 20 Wikipedia Featured Article topics. Under the same generation backend, GEIS improves over the Tasi Harness default writer by 8.0 points on a 100-point PDF quality rubric and outperforms STORM on the two comparable writing dimensions, structural quality and content quality. In the 20-topic improvement experiment, the patched writing skill raises the average score from 82.90 to 86.95, with 17 out of 20 topics improved and the gain mainly coming from content quality. These results show that long-form generation can be reframed from a fixed workflow into an inspectable, modular, and evaluation-guided improvement loop.

</details>


### [77] [Dzongkha Next Word Prediction System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11515)
*Prerna Chhetri,Tenzin Yoezer,Phuntsho Wangmo,Tshewang Bomjan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对宗卡语（不丹官方语言）输入效率低下的问题，提出了基于深度学习（LSTM、Bi-LSTM、GRU）的单词预测系统。实验表明，GRU模型以74.03%的准确率最优，有效减少了击键次数并解决了过拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宗卡语作为不丹国语，其书写因文字复杂、每音节需多次击键且缺乏高效输入工具而耗时费力，亟需一个能即时预测并显示候选词的输入系统来提升打字效率。

Method: 使用DCDD数据集（10万句子、133万词汇），经过去除字母数字字符、分词、生成N-gram序列和填充等预处理，分别训练LSTM、Bi-LSTM和GRU三个模型，并进行超参数微调。

Result: GRU模型表现最佳，准确率达74.03%，且有效避免了过拟合问题。

Conclusion: GRU因其轻量级特性及对大数据集的处理能力，在宗卡语单词预测任务中优于LSTM和Bi-LSTM，为实现便捷的宗卡语输入提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dzongkha, being the national language of Bhutan, is a common and widely spoken language in the country. Official documents, scriptures and other literature products are written in Dzongkha in order to retain the cultural value. However, documenting Dzongkha writing is a challenging and time-consuming process, largely due to the complexity of the script, the need for multiple keystrokes per syllable, and the limited availability of efficient typing tools. An immediate system that can predict and display a list of probable words for Dzongkha is the solution for this problem. The project is mainly aimed to make Dzongkha typing as convenient as possible by reducing the number of keystrokes. Our dataset is acquired from DCDD and has a total of 100000 sentences, 1331282 words and 28344 unique words. The data preprocessing was done by removing all the alphanumeric characters, tokenization, generating N-gram sequences and padding. Three models selected for training are LSTM, Bi-LSTM and GRU. The training process included fine-tuning of the model's hyperparameters. GRU being lightweight and able to handle larger datasets performed best with 74.03% accuracy and also solved the problem of overfitting.

</details>


### [78] [PaperRouter-Agent: A Content-Grounded LLM Agent for Personalized Hierarchical Paper Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11564)
*Keshen Zhou,Lintao Wang,Suqin Yuan,Zhuqiang Lu,Yu Luo,Zhiyong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PaperRouter-Agent，一种无需训练的 LLM 代理，用于个性化分层论文路由（PHPR）任务——将新论文路由到用户私有的文件夹层次结构中。该代理通过基于文件夹成员内容（而非仅名称）进行路由决策，先缩小候选层次、检索证据、检查成员论文并整合用户拒绝反馈。在真实个人图书馆和公共基准上，Recall@1 从 0.39 提升至 0.61，Recall@3 从 0.57 提升至 0.83，LaMP-2 准确率从 44.5% 提升至 51.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人员通常将论文组织成个人文件夹层次结构，现有方法无法处理这种个性化、演化中的、含义多样的分类体系。标准分层文本分类不适用于此场景，因为文件夹含义是用户特定的（主题、缩写、会议、流程等），且由已有论文内容定义。

Method: 提出 PaperRouter-Agent，一种无需训练的 LLM 代理，核心思想是基于文件夹成员内容（而非文件名）进行路由决策。具体包括：(1) 缩小候选层次结构；(2) 检索文件夹特定证据；(3) 通过检查成员论文验证适配性；(4) 整合基于相似度的历史用户拒绝反馈。

Result: 在真实个人图书馆上：Recall@1 从 0.39 提升至 0.61，Recall@3 从 0.57 提升至 0.83；元数据定义的组织类文件夹提升最大（Recall@1 从 0.09 到 0.50）。在 LaMP-2 基准上：准确率从 44.5% 提升至 51.5%，macro-F1 提升 9.0 个点。

Conclusion: PaperRouter-Agent 有效解决了个性化分层论文路由问题，通过基于文件夹成员内容的 LLM 代理方法显著优于单次分类基线，尤其适用于元数据定义的组织类文件夹，且保持低计算成本，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Researchers organize the papers they collect into personal folder hierarchies in reference managers, and route each new paper into the folder where it belongs. This task differs from standard hierarchical text classification. A user's folder hierarchy is not a fixed, shared taxonomy but a private and evolving folksonomy whose folder meanings may be topical, shorthand, venue-based, or process-oriented, and are often defined by the papers already stored inside them. We formalize this setting as personalized hierarchical paper routing (PHPR): assigning an incoming paper to folders in a user-specific hierarchy without per-user training. We propose PaperRouter-Agent, a training-free LLM agent that grounds routing decisions in folder members rather than folder names alone. The agent first narrows the candidate hierarchy, retrieves folder-specific evidence, verifies fit by inspecting member papers, and incorporates similarity-gated feedback from past user rejections. A formative study on real personal libraries shows that PaperRouter-Agent raises overall Recall@1 from 0.39 to 0.61 and Recall@3 from 0.57 to 0.83, with the largest gains on organizational folders defined by metadata such as venue or year, where single-shot methods collapses (Recall@1 0.09 to 0.50). On the public LaMP-2 benchmark, the same approach improves accuracy from 44.5% to 51.5% (+9.0 macro-F1) over a single-shot baseline, while remaining low-cost for practical use.

</details>


### [79] [Beyond Benchmarks: Exposing the Hidden Crisis in Bangla Hate Speech Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11597)
*Faria Afrin Tisha,Fariya Tabassum,Hafsa Binte Kibria,Md. Nahiduzzaman,Mominul Ahsan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了孟加拉语仇恨言论检测系统在隐晦、文化嵌入和表情符号依赖表达上的泛化危机，基准模型在外部验证集上F1分数从91.4%降至75.3%，隐晦仇恨言论检测仅63.4%，表情符号感知预处理可提升12%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论在社交媒体平台传播对在线安全和伦理审核构成重大挑战，尤其在孟加拉语等资源匮乏语言中，由于文化语境、隐晦表达和非正式语言模式，自动检测仍然困难。本研究旨在揭露孟加拉语仇恨言论检测系统的危机，诊断基准训练模型为何无法识别隐晦、依赖语境的仇恨言论。

Method: 训练了六种架构（FastText+CNN、FastText+LSTM、FastText+BiLSTM、BanglaBERT、BanglaBERT+CNN、BanglaBERT+BiLSTM），使用基准数据集（约75,000条帖子）和多源合并数据集（约120,000条帖子），并在从Facebook、Twitter和YouTube收集的标注数据集（约200条帖子）上进行外部验证，仇恨言论进一步分类为显式和隐晦。

Result: BanglaBERT在基准数据集上F1分数达91.4%，但在外部验证集降至75.3%，隐晦仇恨言论（涉及讽刺和表情符号）仅63.4%。FastText+CNN准确率从78.0%降至51.2%。表情符号感知预处理使隐晦仇恨言论检测提升高达12%，而移除表情符号导致性能显著下降（F1：0.75降至0.63）。政治敏感或讽刺评论中频繁出现误分类，暴露过度审查风险。

Conclusion: 本研究不仅暴露了因隐晦、文化嵌入和表情符号依赖表达导致的泛化危机，还强调了开发自适应、表情符号感知和文化扎根框架的必要性，以确保伦理审核同时维护言论自由。研究结果为研究者、社交媒体平台和政策制定者设计更上下文敏感的、面向低资源语言的仇恨言论检测系统提供了洞见。

Abstract: The spread of hate speech (HS) across different social media platforms (SMPs) poses a major concern for online safety and ethical moderation. Automatic detection of HS remains a challenging task, especially in under-resourced languages like Bangla, due to cultural context, implicit expressions, and informal linguistic patterns. This study aimed to expose the crisis of Bangla HS detection systems by diagnosing how and why benchmark-trained models fail to identify implicit, context-dependent HS. Six architectures (FastText + CNN, FastText + LSTM, FastText + BiLSTM, BanglaBERT, BanglaBERT + CNN, and BanglaBERT + BiLSTM) were trained on benchmark datasets (about 75,000 posts) and a merged multi-source dataset (about 120,000 posts), then externally validated on an annotated dataset (about 200 posts) collected from Facebook, Twitter, and YouTube, labeled as HS and non-HS, where HS was further categorized as explicit and implicit. BanglaBERT achieved an F1-score of 91.4% on benchmark datasets but declined to 75.3% on the external set and 63.4% for implicit HS involving sarcasm and emojis. The accuracy of FastText + CNN dropped from 78.0% to 51.2% under similar conditions. Emoji-aware preprocessing improved implicit HS detection by up to 12%, whereas emoji removal caused a notable decline in performance (F1: 0.75 to 0.63). Frequent misclassifications in politically charged or satirical comments revealed over-policing risks. This study not only exposes the generalization crisis due to implicit, culturally embedded, and emoji-laden expressions but also underscores the need for developing adaptive, emoji-aware, and culturally grounded frameworks that ensure ethical moderation while preserving freedom of expression. Findings of this study provide insights for researchers, SMPs, and policymakers to design more context-sensitive HS detection systems for low-resource languages.

</details>


### [80] [Globally Consistent Coloring Schemes for Language Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11606)
*Moses Charikar,Jon Kleinberg,Chirag Pabbaraju*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在Gold语言识别极限模型中，仅用每个字符串末尾一个比特的颜色标注（终端着色），就能使任意可数无限语言集合被识别。作者证明存在一个与集合无关的通用双色终端着色方案，但该构造需要超限递归，且证明任何有限色Borel可定义终端着色都无法覆盖所有可数子集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Gold-Angluin结果表明许多自然语言集合无法在极限识别模型中被学习。近期工作通过跟踪着色（trace coloring）克服了这一障碍，但需要为每个符号赋予颜色。本文探究是否可以将着色压缩到每个字符串仅末尾一个颜色（终端着色），从而最小化额外信息量。

Method: 采用超限递归（transfinite recursion）构造全局终端着色方案，使得同一预分配的双色终端着色能识别所有可数无限语言子集合。同时使用Borel映射形式化可构造性概念，通过不可约性论证证明任何有限色Borel可定义终端着色无法识别所有可数子集合。

Result: 每个字符串末尾一个比特的终端着色足以使任意可数无限语言集合可被极限识别；存在集合无关的通用双色终端着色方案；该构造必须是非构造性的（超限递归），任何有限色Borel可定义终端着色都无法实现全局覆盖；而已知的跟踪着色构造编码为终端着色时虽是Borel的，但需要无限多种颜色。

Conclusion: 语言学习的极限识别模型中，额外信息可被极致压缩至每个例子末尾一比特，但全局方案的普适性必须以非构造性为代价，这揭示了可学习性与可构造性之间的根本张力。

Abstract: We study how little extra information is needed to make adversarial language learning possible. In Gold's model of language identification in the limit, a learner is given an enumeration of the strings from an unknown language chosen from a countable language collection. The learner guesses the identity of the language over the course of the enumeration, and it succeeds if, eventually, all of its guesses are the correct language. Classical results of Gold and Angluin show that many natural collections cannot be learned in this way. Recent work on trace colorings, motivated by the success of thinking-trace strategies in language learning, overcomes this obstruction by annotating every symbol of every string with a color. We ask whether the learner really needs this whole sequence of colors, or whether one color at the end of each string (a terminal coloring) is enough for language identification.
  We show that just one terminal bit per string is enough for every countable collection of infinite languages. In fact, the colorings can be chosen collection-independently: there is a single assignment of a two-color terminal coloring to every infinite language such that the same preassigned colorings identify every countable subcollection. Thus, in this model, an entire color trace can be compressed to one bit attached to the end of each example.
  Our global construction uses transfinite recursion, and we prove that this kind of nonconstructivity is unavoidable for any bounded number of colors. As a notion of constructivity, we use the formalism of Borel maps (a regularity condition satisfied by natural explicit constructions); we show that no global terminal coloring with a finite number of colors defined by a Borel map can identify all countable subcollections. By contrast, known trace-coloring constructions are Borel when encoded as terminal colorings, but require infinitely many colors.

</details>


### [81] [Extending LLM Context via Associative Recurrent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11614)
*Gleb Kuzmin,Ivan Rodkin,Aydar Bulatov,Yuri Kuratov,Lyudmila Rvanova,Mikhail Katkov,Ilia Sochenkov,Misha Tsodyks,Timothy Baldwin,Mikhail Burtsev,Artem Shelmanov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了关联循环记忆变换器（ARMT）作为一种扩展大语言模型上下文长度的实用方法，实现了线性存储扩展和更好的效率。通过构建两个领域特定的长上下文数据集、提出包含持续预训练、合成数据生成、课程学习和选择性记忆集成的完整训练方案，实验表明ARMT增强模型能处理远超原始上下文限制的输入，在分布外长度上泛化更有效，并在原始窗口内节省30% FLOPs的同时保持基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展大语言模型的上下文长度对许多实际应用至关重要，但标准变换器受限于二次方计算和线性存储扩展，亟需更高效的长上下文处理方案。

Method: 构建两个领域特定的长上下文数据集；提出ARMT扩展上下文的完整训练方案，包括持续预训练、合成长上下文数据生成、课程学习以及将关联记忆选择性集成到特定模型层；在多个基准上进行实验评估。

Result: ARMT增强模型能处理远超原始上下文限制的输入而不降低性能；能更有效地泛化到分布外上下文长度；在原始上下文窗口内使用30%更少的FLOPs仍能保持基线性能。

Conclusion: 关联循环记忆变换器（ARMT）是一种实用且高效的大语言模型上下文扩展方法，具有恒定的存储扩展和更好的计算效率。

Abstract: Extending the context length of large language models (LLMs) is critical for many real-world applications, yet standard transformers remain constrained by quadratic compute and linear memory scaling. In this work, we investigate the Associative Recurrent Memory Transformer (ARMT) as a practical approach for enabling long-context processing in LLMs, constant memory scaling, and better efficiency. We make three main contributions. First, we construct two domain-specific long-context datasets designed to evaluate realistic workloads, focusing on narrow-domain fine-tuning scenarios. Second, we propose a comprehensive training recipe for ARMT-based context extension, combining continued pre-training, synthetic long-context data generation, curriculum learning, and selective integration of associative memory into chosen model layers. Third, we present an extensive experimental study demonstrating that ARMT-augmented models: (i) process inputs well beyond their original context limits without degrading performance relative to in-limit baselines; (ii) generalize more effectively to out-of-distribution context lengths; and (iii) need 30% less FLOPs while preserving baseline performance within the original context window.

</details>


### [82] [Losing My Composure: Predicting Compositionality Over Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11667)
*Chris Jenkins,Emma Raimundo Schulz,Filip Miletić,Sabine Schulte im Walde*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了英语和德语名词复合词语义变化及组合性随时间演变的趋势，构建了历时组合性评分数据集，提出了组合性趋势预测任务。实验发现组合性随时间仅呈微弱下降趋势，窄时间窗口训练的模型与人工评分更吻合，静态表征在预测任务中与上下文表征竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献提出复合词应随时间推移变得组合性更弱（透明度更低）的假设，但该假设缺乏历时语料上的实证检验。作者旨在系统调查并建模复合词组合性在数十年间的渐变过程。

Method: (1) 构建包含23个德语和26个英语目标复合词的历时组合性评分数据集，提供每十年评分及趋势标签；(2) 训练约100个不同时间粒度（1-5十年窗口）的语义向量表示模型；(3) 在组合性趋势预测任务上比较静态表征与上下文表征的表现。

Result: (1) 组合性随时间仅呈现微弱的负向趋势，与文献中强烈倾向相反；(2) 窄时间窗口（单十年或增量扩展窗口）训练的模型比整半个世纪窗口的模型更符合人工评分；(3) 静态表征在组合性趋势预测任务中与上下文表征竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 历时组合性变化并非如假设的那样显著，窄粒度时间窗口建模优于粗粒度窗口，且静态表征在特定历时任务中并不逊色于上下文表征。

Abstract: We explore the phenomenon of semantic change of German and English noun compounds, with the objective of investigating and modeling gradual changes of meanings and degrees of compositionality in the past and over time. To do so, we introduce the Compositionality Trend Prediction task, which is evaluated against a novel dataset of in-context compositionality ratings sampled across several decades of diachronic corpora for 23 German and 26 English target compounds, uniquely providing per-decade ratings and corresponding trends over time. These per-decade compositionality ratings allow us to investigate empirically untested hypotheses of generalized trends in compositionality over time, such as the idea that compounds should become less compositional (less transparent) over time. Beyond our empirical observations from the diachronic compositionality annotations, we perform experiments with semantic vector representations of varying complexity, as well as several temporal granularities for training these representations on diachronic data, resulting in about 100 models of each representation type, each covering a different 1--5 decade slice of a diachronic corpus. Contrary to the decisive tendency posited in the literature, we find only a small negative trend in compositionality over time in our target compounds. In our computational experiments, we find that using models trained on narrow time slices of diachronic data (single decades, or incrementally expanding temporal windows) align better with the per-decade compositionality ratings than those trained on an entire half-century window, the latter setting being an analog for the prevalent modeling approach of training representations on an entire half of a corpus' data. Additionally, we find static representations to be competitive with contextual representations in the Compositionality Trend Prediction task.

</details>


### [83] [RAGU: A Multi-Step GraphRAG Engine with a Compact Domain-Adapted LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11683)
*Mikhail Komarov,Ivan Bondarenko,Stanislav Shtuka,Oleg Sedukhin,Roman Shuvalov,Yana Dementyeva,Matvey Solovyov,Nikolay O. Nikitin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RAGU是一个开源模块化GraphRAG引擎，通过分离提取与合并、两阶段类型化提取、DBSCAN去重、LLM摘要和Leiden社区检测来改进知识图谱构建。其核心见解是：流水线中LLM所需的语言技能（理解、提取、推理）对模型大小的依赖远弱于事实性世界知识。基于此训练的Meno-Lite-0.1（7B模型）在知识图谱构建上超越Qwen2.5-32B（+12.5%调和平均），在GraphRAG任务上与之持平。在医学GraphRAG-Bench上，RAGU在证据召回（最高0.84 vs ≤0.76）和综合任务上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphRAG系统采用单次提取构建知识图谱，产生噪声实体和脆弱检索，亟需更鲁棒的图增强检索生成方法。

Method: 提出RAGU引擎，核心包括：(1) 分离提取与合并的流程：两阶段类型化提取、DBSCAN去重、LLM摘要、Leiden社区检测；(2) 核心见解：语言技能（理解、提取、推理）随模型大小增长弱于事实知识；(3) 基于此训练Meno-Lite-0.1（7B参数），专注于语言技能优化。

Result: Meno-Lite-0.1在知识图谱构建上超越Qwen2.5-32B（+12.5%调和平均），在英文GraphRAG任务上持平；在医学GraphRAG-Bench上证据召回达0.84（对比≤0.76），综合任务超越HippoRAG2；多跳事实性QA中HippoRAG2的优势被证明主要来自答案格式伪影。

Conclusion: RAGU通过分离提取与合并策略有效提升GraphRAG质量；语言技能型小模型可在图构建上超越大模型，挑战了"越大越好"的范式；系统开源可复现。

Abstract: Graph retrieval-augmented generation (GraphRAG) enhances large language models with structured knowledge, yet existing systems construct knowledge graphs in a single extraction pass, producing noisy entities and brittle retrieval. RAGU, an open-source modular GraphRAG engine, addresses this by separating extraction from consolidation: entities and relations pass through two-stage typed extraction, DBSCAN-backed deduplication, LLM summarization, and Leiden community detection. A key insight motivates a compact extractor: the skills an in-pipeline LLM needs - comprehension, extraction, reasoning over context - are language skills that grow only weakly with model size, unlike factual world knowledge. Accordingly, we train Meno-Lite-0.1, a 7B model optimized for language skills, which outperforms Qwen2.5-32B on knowledge-graph construction (+12.5% relative harmonic mean) and matches it on English GraphRAG tasks. On GraphRAG-Bench (Medical), RAGU retrieves the most complete context at every factoid level (evidence recall up to 0.84 vs. $\leq$0.76) and overtakes HippoRAG2 on synthesis tasks; on multi-hop factoid QA, the apparent HippoRAG2 advantage is shown to be largely an answer-format artifact. RAGU is installable via $\texttt{pip install graph_ragu}$, runs on a single GPU, and is released under MIT. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/RaguTeam/RAGU, and the Meno-Lite-0.1 model can be obtained from https://huggingface.co/bond005/meno-lite-0.1.

</details>


### [84] [Production and Perception in LLMs: A Token Probability Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11703)
*Anna Marklová,Jiří Milička,Martina Vokáčová,Rudolf Rosa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过直接测量token概率而非元语言提示，系统验证了仅通过提示框架就能在纯解码器LLM中诱发生成-感知区分，实验在多个开源模型上稳定复现，生成-感知距离约为生成-生成距离的1.8倍，且感知提示的影响在序列起始处最强并随时间衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理语言学中语言生成与感知的不对称性已被充分证实，但LLM是否表现出类似的功能性区分尚不清楚，因为LLM在输入和输出处理中使用相同的底层机制（下一个token预测）。本研究旨在探究这一开放问题。

Method: 使用Llama-3.1-8B等五个开源模型，通过直接token概率测量（而非元语言提示）来操作化生成-感知区分。在生成提示下生成诗歌，然后在改写后的生成提示和感知导向提示下重新评分相同token。实验包含四种生成提示和三种感知提示，并进行了时间序列分析。

Result: 生成-感知距离始终且显著大于生成-生成距离，平均比率约为1.8，且条件间范围无重叠。生成-生成对照条件下的近天花板相关性证实该效应源于交际框架而非提示表面变化。该效应在五个开源模型上均复现。时间分析显示感知提示在序列起始处影响最强，随生成上下文积累而衰减。

Conclusion: 仅通过提示框架就能在纯解码器架构的LLM中诱发生成-感知区分，表明LLM概率分布中存在与心理语言学类似的功能性不对称。

Abstract: The asymmetry between language production and perception has been well-documented in psycholinguistics. Whether large language models (LLMs) exhibit a functionally analogous distinction remains an open question, particularly given that LLMs rely on the same underlying mechanism (next-token prediction) for both input and output processing. In this exploratory study, we operationalize the production-perception distinction through direct token probability measurements rather than metalinguistic prompting. Using the base Llama-3.1-8B model, we generated poems under a production prompt and re-scored the same tokens under both rephrased production prompts and perception-oriented prompts. Across an extended experiment with four production and three perception prompts, production-perception distances consistently and substantially exceeded production-production distances, with non-overlapping ranges across conditions and an overall average ratio of approximately 1.8. Near-ceiling correlations in the production-production control confirm that the effect is specific to communicative framing rather than prompt surface variation, and we show the effect replicates across five open-weight models (Llama-3.1-8B, EuroLLM-9B, gemma-2-9b-it, Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3, and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct), spanning both base and instruction-tuned variants. Temporal analysis revealed that the perception prompt exerts its strongest influence at the beginning of the sequence, with divergence decaying as generated context accumulates, though the specific shape of this decay varies across prompt pairs. These findings suggest that prompt framing alone induces a production-perception distinction in LLM probability distributions, even within a decoder-only architecture.

</details>


### [85] [JobHop v2: A Large-Scale Career Trajectory Dataset from Unstructured Resumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11715)
*Iman Johary,Guillaume Bied,Alexandru C. Mara,Tijl De Bie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: JobHop v2 是一个从约44万份多语种简历中提取的大规模职业轨迹数据集，包含355,315条带ESCO编码、季度时间信息和教育水平的轨迹，通过改进的LLM提取管道实现100% JSON解析率，在标注一致性方面接近人类标注者上限（差距仅1.1-2.7个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的职业轨迹数据集要么规模小、不公开，要么使用预标准化职业代码和LLM合成的非真实文本，缺乏大规模、公开可用且基于真实自由文本的丰富标注数据集，难以支持可复现的职业轨迹研究。

Method: 基于约44万份VDAB提供的假名化多语种简历，构建端到端LLM提取管道，采用推理控制的LLM推理与重试机制（实现100% JSON解析率），设计更丰富的提取模式，并建立与三个互补标注基线对比的评估协议。

Result: 发布了355,315条职业轨迹，包含ESCO职业代码、季度级时间信息和五级教育水平标注。最佳提取器在所有对比模型中最接近标注者间一致性上限，差距仅1.1-2.7个百分点。

Conclusion: JobHop v2通过改进的LLM提取管道大幅提升了数据集规模和标注丰富度，达到了接近人类标注者水平的性能，并公开数据集和代码以支持可复现的职业轨迹研究。

Abstract: Large-scale, richly annotated career trajectory data underpins workforce planning, job recommendation, and labour market analysis, yet publicly available datasets are either small, closed to independent use, or built from pre-standardized occupational codes with LLM-synthesized rather than authentic free text. We present JobHop~v2, an improved version of the publicly available JobHop dataset, constructed through end-to-end large language model (LLM) extraction from a corpus of ${\sim}440{,}000$ pseudonymized, multilingual resumes provided by VDAB, the Flemish Public Employment Service. The released dataset comprises $355{,}315$ career trajectories annotated with ESCO occupational codes, quarter-level temporal information, and normalized five-level education attainment, broadening both the coverage and the annotation richness of the original release. Relative to v1, JobHop~v2 introduces a redesigned extraction pipeline based on reasoning-controlled LLM inference with a retry mechanism (achieving a 100% JSON parse rate), a richer extraction schema, and a revised evaluation protocol scored against three complementary annotation baselines. Evaluated against these baselines, our best extractor comes closest to the inter-annotator agreement ceiling among all compared models, trailing it by only 1.1-2.7 percentage points. The dataset and code are publicly released to support reproducible career-trajectory research.

</details>


### [86] [STEP: Career-Path Recommendation via Temporal and Educational Trajectory Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11722)
*Iman Johary,Guillaume Bied,Alexandru C. Mara,Tijl De Bie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出STEP系统，利用时间衰减GRU、基于教育程度的特征线性调制（FiLM）和注意力池化，从非结构化简历中预测职业轨迹中的下一个职位；同时引入ROUTE两阶段对比学习（无监督去噪自编码+有监督对比微调）来优化职业表示，在多个数据集上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简历数据富含职业轨迹信息，但其非结构化、异构、多语言的特性一直阻碍大规模系统分析。大语言模型的出现使得从非结构化简历中提取包含时间和教育信号的职业轨迹数据成为可能，从而为职业路径推荐创造新机遇。

Method: STEP系统：采用时间衰减GRU单元建模时间动态，利用基于教育程度的FiLM进行特征线性调制，通过注意力池化选择相关特征用于下一个职位预测。ROUTE模块：两阶段对比学习，先通过无监督去噪自编码使多语言编码器适应职业领域，再进行有监督对比微调并引入引导式负样本选择。

Result: 在四个职业轨迹数据集（包括改进版JobHop数据集）上，STEP在下一个职位预测任务中优于现有最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: STEP系统成功利用时间和教育信号实现了有效的职业路径推荐，且数据集和代码已公开以支持可复现的职业轨迹研究。

Abstract: Career paths encode decades of skill acquisition, role transitions, and educational investment, and understanding them at scale underpins workforce planning, labor market policy, and job recommendation. Resumes are a rich source of information about career paths: they contain detailed descriptions of work experience, education, and skills. Yet their unstructured, heterogeneous, and multilingual nature has long prevented large-scale systematic analysis. With the advent of large language models (LLMs), it is now possible to source rich career trajectory data containing temporal and educational signals from unstructured resumes, enabling new opportunities for career-path recommendation. Exploiting this opportunity, we present STEP (Sequential Trajectory of Employment Prediction), a novel career-path recommendation system that leverages temporal and educational signals to predict the next job in a career trajectory. STEP integrates a time-decay Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU) cell to model temporal dynamics, Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) conditioned on educational attainment, and attention-based sequence pooling to select relevant features for next job prediction. To improve internal occupation representation for STEP, we introduce ROUTE, a two-stage contrastive procedure that first adapts a multilingual encoder to the career domain via unsupervised denoising autoencoding, then performs supervised contrastive fine-tuning with guided negative selection. We evaluate STEP on four datasets of career trajectories, including an improved version of our publicly available JobHop dataset, and show that it outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in next job prediction. The dataset and code are publicly released to support reproducible career-trajectory research.

</details>


### [87] [MET: Theory-Grounded and Culture-Aware Multilingual Moral Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11736)
*Ayoung Lee,Ryan Kwon,Yunxiang Zhang,Yuxuan Liu,Peter Railton,Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对多语言道德决策中的三个空白（评估基准缺乏文化适应性、推理方法基于英语中心静态框架且缺乏理论支撑、训练方法依赖昂贵监督），提出了三项贡献：多语言道德决策基准MCLASH、基于理论的两步提示方法MET，以及无需外部监督的自蒸馏训练方法MET-D。实验表明MET-D在多个模型上平均提升3.71-4.23个F1点，并将本地语言推理提升62.13点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作在多语言道德决策中忽视了三个关键方面：1）多语言评估基准直接翻译，未能适配文化特异性内容；2）推理阶段方法依赖静态、英语中心的框架且缺乏道德理论基础；3）训练方法需要昂贵的强模型或人工标注监督。

Method: 1) 提出MCLASH基准，捕捉文化情境化的道德直觉和社会规范；2) 提出MET方法，通过专家构建的心理学和哲学理论基础，先选择情境和文化特异的理论依据，再以用户母语进行推理；3) 提出MET-D，在MET第二步引入自蒸馏训练阶段，无需外部监督。

Result: MET-D在Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B、Gemma3-4B三个模型上平均提升MCLASH 3.71分、MMoralExceptQA 4.23分；马来语在Qwen3-8B上MCLASH提升达12.94分；本地语言推理平均提升62.13分。

Conclusion: 这些贡献为文化对齐、理论支撑的多语言道德推理开辟了道路，揭示了不同文化中有利的理论依据存在系统性差异。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used for moral decision-making across diverse linguistic and cultural contexts, yet existing work overlooks multilinguality on three aspects: 1) multilingual evaluation benchmarks use direct translation, failing to adapt culture-specific items; 2) inference-time methods for moral reasoning rely on static, English-centric scaffolds and lack grounding in moral theory; 3) training methods for moral decision-making typically require expensive supervision from stronger models or human annotators. We address these gaps with three contributions. First, we introduce MCLASH, a multilingual moral decision-making benchmark to capture culturally situated moral intuitions and social norms across languages. Second, we propose MET (Multilingual Ethics with Theory-grounded reasoning), a two-step prompting method built on expert-curated, theory-based grounds drawn from psychology and philosophy: the model first selects situation- and culture-specific grounds, then reasons over them in the native language of the user. Third, we introduce MET-D (MET-Distillation), which enhances the second step through a self-distillation training stage that requires no external supervision. MET-D improves macro-F1 over the base model on all three models of different sizes and families (Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, Gemma3-4B), by an average of 3.71 points on MCLASH and 4.23 on MMoralExceptQA, with a peak MCLASH gain of 12.94 points for Malay on Qwen3-8B. We further reveal that MET-D increases native-language reasoning by 62.13 points on average, and that beneficial grounds differ systematically across cultures. Together, these contributions open the path for culture-aligned, theory-grounded multilingual moral reasoning.

</details>


### [88] [How Temperature Shapes Ideological Discourse in Retrieval-Augmented Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11783)
*Elmira Salari,Hazem Amamou,José Victor de Souza,Shruti Kshirsagar,Maria Nunes Delfino,Anderson Avila*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了检索增强生成（RAG）框架在包含意识形态话语时，如何将意识形态偏见传递到LLM输出中。通过对1,117篇COVID-19治疗文章进行词汇多维分析（LMDA），识别出三种意识形态话语，并让多个LLM在不同采样温度下回答意识形态问题。结果发现RAG容易将意识形态话语转移到LLM回答中，且中等温度下话语对齐程度最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽然关注了RAG对检索错误的鲁棒性，但忽视了检索材料中的意识形态偏见对LLM输出的影响——即RAG可能传播、放大或抑制意识形态话语。

Method: 采用词汇多维分析（LMDA）对1,117篇COVID-19治疗文章进行语料分析，识别三种意识形态话语；以此作为RAG的外部知识源；测试多个LLM在不同采样温度下回答意识形态问题；对生成文本进行语义和词汇层面的相似性评估。

Result: RAG框架会将意识形态话语转移到LLM回答中；采样温度对转移强度有可测量的影响；中等温度下话语对齐程度最高，低温下话语转移减弱。

Conclusion: RAG框架容易传递检索材料中的意识形态偏见，采样温度是影响该传递强度的关键因素，过高的确定性会抑制话语转移。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has been increasingly adopted to reduce hallucinations and strengthen the factual grounding of large language models (LLMs). While robustness to errors in the retrieval process has been explored, the impact of ideological bias on LLM outputs has been overlooked. For instance, if the retrieved material contains ideological positions, the RAG may transmit, amplify, or suppress such ideological discourses in its outputs. In this study, we address this issue by examining the influence of the RAG framework, comprising ideological discourses, in LLM-generated answers. To this end, we applied Lexical Multidimensional Analysis (LMDA) on a corpus of 1,117 COVID-19 treatment articles, identifying three ideological discourses. This corpus is then used as the external knowledge source for the RAG. We assessed several LLMs by having the models answer ideological questions at different sampling temperatures. The generated texts were assessed semantically and lexically based on their similarities with ideological reference texts. Our findings show that the RAG framework is prone to transferring ideological discourses into LLM responses, with sampling temperature having a measurable impact on the strength of this transfer. Discoursive alignment between generated answers and the reference text is highest at moderate temperatures, where models balance stochasticity with retrieval grounding, and drops at low temperatures, indicating that overly deterministic sampling suppresses discourse transfer.

</details>


### [89] [Introducing Human-Centeredness in AI-Assisted Lexicography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11808)
*Antonio San Martin,Catherine Trekker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个以人为中心的人工智能（HCAI）框架，用于AI辅助词典编纂，主张AI应增强而非取代词典编纂者，结合高自动化水平与有意义的人类控制，并关注增强型词典编纂者、社会技术背景、偏见及工具设计四个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI为词典编纂带来机遇的同时，也引发了关于词典编纂者未来角色以及语言文化多样性保护的担忧。

Method: 借鉴HCAI原则及在其它语言职业中的应用，识别并构建了四个相互关联的维度（增强型词典编纂者、AI整合的社会技术背景、偏见、AI驱动的词典编纂工具设计）作为分析框架。

Result: 提出了一个四维度HCAI框架，强调AI应增强而非取代词典编纂者，需保持高自动化与人类控制的平衡，维护专业自主性，缓解AI生成偏见，并以词典编纂者需求为中心设计工具。

Conclusion: 该框架为未来研究和AI在词典编纂工作流中的有益整合提供了基础。

Abstract: This paper proposes a human-centered artificial intelligence (HCAI) framework for AI-assisted lexicography. While generative AI offers significant opportunities to enhance lexicographic work, it also raises concerns regarding the future role of lexicographers and the preservation of linguistic and cultural diversity. Drawing on HCAI principles and previous applications in other language professions, the paper identifies four interrelated dimensions through which AI integration in lexicography can be understood and critically examined: the augmented lexicographer, the sociotechnical context of AI integration, bias, and the design of AI-powered lexicographic tools. The framework argues that AI should augment rather than replace lexicographers, combining high levels of automation with meaningful human control. It further emphasizes the importance of preserving professional agency, mitigating AI-generated biases, and designing tools around the needs of lexicographers. By doing so, the paper provides a foundation for future research and the beneficial integration of AI into lexicographic workflows.

</details>


### [90] [AdvancedMathBench: A Benchmark Suite for Advanced Mathematical Proof Generation and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11849)
*Lingkai Kong,Zijian Wu,Yuzhe Gu,Haiteng Zhao,Wenyong Huang,Shuang Sun,Zhicheng Xiong,Xiaotian Zhang,Shuya Zhao,Yan Wang,Disheng Xu,Wenwei Zhang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AdvancedMathBench基准套件评估大模型高级数学推理能力，包含ProverBench（296道本科至博士资格考试级别证明题）和VerifierBench（888道模型生成证明轨迹及其专家标注）。实验表明最先进模型在证明生成上最高仅达75.8分，证明验证Balanced F1仅65.1，关键错误检测是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学基准在高级数学领域覆盖范围有限且评估粒度粗，多依赖最终答案正确性而忽略推理过程有效性评估，无法全面衡量LLMs在高级数学（如本科及博士资格考试级别）的真实推理能力。

Method: 构建AdvancedMathBench基准，包含ProverBench（296道跨学科证明题）和VerifierBench（888条模型生成证明轨迹+专家标注）；开发自动验证流水线，基于大规模专家标注训练，可输出正确性判决和细粒度错误评估。

Result: ProverBench上GPT-5.5-xhigh在UGD和QE分项上仅达75.8和66.1；VerifierBench上最佳模型Balanced F1仅65.1，模型普遍低真负率，关键错误检测能力不足。

Conclusion: AdvancedMathBench对前沿模型仍具挑战性，高级数学证明构建和验证能力均有显著提升空间，尤其关键错误检测是当前主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable performance on high-school and olympiad-style mathematics, yet their capabilities on advanced mathematics remain poorly understood. Existing benchmarks, however, fall short in both scope and evaluation granularity: they provide limited disciplinary coverage and often rely on final-answer correctness or coarse judgments, leaving the validity of the reasoning process inadequately assessed. To bridge this gap, we introduce AdvancedMathBench, a benchmark suite designed to evaluate advanced mathematical reasoning capabilities. Its core proof-generation benchmark, ProverBench, contains 296 problems spanning undergraduate and doctoral qualifying-exam levels. To provide reliable evaluation of the proofs, we develop a dedicated automatic verification pipeline trained on large-scale expert annotations to produce both correctness verdicts and fine-grained assessments of proof errors, which exhibits strong agreement with human experts on held-out proof trajectories. We further introduce VerifierBench, consisting of 888 model-generated proof trajectories paired with expert ground truth, to evaluate whether models can correctly judge proof validity and provide sound verification rationales. Experiments show that AdvancedMathBench remains challenging for frontier models. On proof generation, the best-performing model, GPT-5.5-xhigh, achieves only 75.8 and 66.1 on the UGD and QE splits, respectively, indicating substantial room for improvement on advanced mathematical proof construction. On proof verification, the best model attains a Balanced F1 of only 65.1, and models generally exhibit low true negative rates, suggesting that critical error detection remains a major bottleneck.

</details>


### [91] [A Durability and Cross-Language Transfer Benchmark for a Validated Teaching-Feedback Classification Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11873)
*Esteban U. Vega Barajas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究重新评估了一个既有的教学评价反馈分类协议，发现其在2019年的冻结嵌入方法虽然在西班牙语任务上表现尚可，但在跨语言迁移和与前沿大语言模型（如2026年模型）的对比中，前沿模型仅在西班牙语主题分类上略优，情感分类无优势，且英语任务上无显著差异。因此模型选择更多是部署决策而非方法属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有教学评价反馈分类协议基于2019年的冻结嵌入设计，存在两个关键问题：1）随着表示学习方法的快速演进，旧协议是否仍具竞争力；2）该协议能否跨语言迁移（西班牙语→英语）。需要验证协议的持久性与可迁移性。

Method: 在原始西班牙语语料上对比三代表示方法：稀疏词特征、冻结Transformer嵌入、提示式大语言模型。同时将情感分类任务迁移至英语，使用4.5万条平衡标注语料，并与方面标注的教育数据集交叉验证。采用描述性配对比较分析。

Result: 2026年前沿模型在西班牙语最难主题分类任务上取得最高F1值，但在情感分类上不优于廉价模型；在英语任务上，前沿模型与廉价模型无描述性分离。协议整体表现出持久性。

Conclusion: 教学评价分类协议具有跨时间、跨语言的持久性；模型选择更多是部署层面的权衡（成本、效率等），而非协议方法的内在属性。

Abstract: Institutions collect far more open-ended teaching-evaluation feedback than they read. A prior study introduced a validated protocol for classifying such comments by thematic category and sentiment, built from a documented annotation guide, an intra-annotator reliability measurement, stratified cross-validation, and a held-out evaluation on a Spanish institutional corpus with a frozen-encoder design. Two questions limit its reuse: whether a protocol fixed to 2019-era frozen embeddings stays competitive as representation methods advance, and whether it transfers to a second language. We re-run it on the original Spanish data across three representation generations, sparse lexical features, frozen transformer embeddings, and prompted large language models, and transfer its sentiment task to English with a balanced 45,000-comment corpus checked against an aspect-labeled education dataset. Treating paired comparisons as descriptive, we find the protocol durable: a 2026 frontier model posts the highest thematic F1 on the hardest Spanish task, yet shows no sentiment advantage over a cheap model and no descriptive separation from it on English, so model choice is a deployment decision, not a property of the method.

</details>


### [92] [Metacognition in LLMs: Foundations, Progress, and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11881)
*Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu,Areeb Gani,Jacqueline Lu,Jordan Thomas,Mark Steyvers,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次全面综述了大语言模型（LLMs）的元认知能力现状，包括评估方法、提升技术、应用前景及未来挑战，并附有系统性论文清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元认知是智能的核心组成部分，对学习、推理和决策至关重要，也是构建透明可信AI系统的关键。尽管LLMs在众多任务上取得了显著进展，但其是否具备、如何具备、以及在多大程度上具备有效元认知能力尚不明确，这一研究空白亟待填补。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，对该新兴领域的研究进行梳理与分类，涵盖评估基准、提升技术、应用实践及研究发现等方面，并整理了可公开获取的论文列表。

Result: 构建了LLMs元认知领域的系统性分类体系；总结出用于测量和评估LLMs元认知能力的方法与基准；归纳了激发、增强及应用LLMs元认知能力的技术路线；揭示了当前研究的主要发现与启示。

Conclusion: LLMs的元认知研究仍处于早期阶段，存在诸多开放问题与挑战，但已在能力提升、系统可靠性和智能透明度方面展现出重要潜力。未来研究应聚焦于更全面的评估框架、更强的元认知训练方法以及实际应用中的落地验证。

Abstract: Metacognition is a foundational component of intelligence critical to effective learning, problem solving, decision-making, communication, and more. In recent years, it has become increasingly recognized as a cornerstone of capable, transparent AI systems. Yet while LLMs have made significant progress across diverse real-world tasks, it is not yet clear when, how, or to what extent they can exhibit or be endowed with effective metacognitive abilities, nor how such abilities can be adapted to advance the fundamental capabilities, reliability, and intelligence of AI systems. This paper bridges this gap by presenting the first comprehensive overview of the current state of knowledge on metacognition for LLMs. We analyze and taxonomize the landscape of this emerging field and summarize recent technical advancements, including methods and benchmarks to measure and evaluate LLMs' metacognitive abilities, techniques to elicit, improve, and apply metacognition in LLMs, and findings and implications of ongoing research. We also discuss applications, open questions and challenges, and promising directions for future work. Our aim is to provide a detailed and up-to-date review of this topic and stimulate meaningful research and discussion. An organized list of papers can be found at https://github.com/yale-nlp/LLM-Metacognition.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [From ML Predictions to Informed Diagnostic Assistance Using the Toulmin Model of Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09664)
*Anca Marginean,Adrian Groza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Toulmin论证模型的可解释性框架，将ML驱动的视网膜诊断分解为主张、依据、担保、限定词、反驳和支撑六个部分，利用生物标志物提取模型、MedGemma医学知识代理、MedSigLip图像相似度等技术为人类专家提供结构化、可解释的诊断评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML诊断模型缺乏结构化的可解释性，传统XAI方法难以提供充分的论据支撑，临床专家难以对AI生成的诊断主张进行批判性评估。

Method: 基于Toulmin论证模型分解诊断过程：1) 生物标志物提取模型提供"依据"；2) MedGemma代理分析"担保"（连接依据与主张的逻辑）；3) 量化评估确定"限定词"；4) MedSigLip计算图像相似度构建"反驳"。所有组件呈现给人类专家以便评估。

Result: 该框架能够将视网膜ML诊断分解为结构化、可解释的论证组件，使人类专家可以更知情、更批判地评估AI生成的诊断。

Conclusion: 基于Toulmin模型的论证方法为ML诊断提供了超越传统XAI的结构化可解释性框架，有助于提升AI辅助诊断的临床可信度和可接受性。

Abstract: To provide a structured and interpretable assessment, we decompose the image-based diagnosis into components following the Toulmin model of argumentation. This model consists of a claim, grounds, warrant, qualifier, rebuttal, and backing. Consider a claim generated by a machine learning (ML) model for retinal diagnosis. Rather than accepting this claim at face value, one could either apply explainable AI (XAI) methods or adopt an argumentation-based approach. In our framework, a model specialized in biomarker extraction from images provides the grounds. The warrant-linking the grounds to the claim - is analyzed by an agent equipped with medical knowledge; in our architecture, this role is fulfilled by a MedGemma agent. The qualifier is determined based on the overall quantitative evaluation of both the warrant and grounds models. Finally, a rebuttal is constructed using image similarity measures computed with MedSigLip. All these components are presented to the human expert, enabling a more informed and critical assessment of the ML-generated diagnosis.

</details>


### [94] [Format Sensitivity Index: Token-Controlled Prompt Wrapper Robustness and Schema Compliance in LLM Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09665)
*Deep Pankajbhai Mehta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现提示词包装（wrapper）的格式差异会导致模型评分发生足以翻转排行榜结论的变化。通过引入格式敏感性指数（FSI）和可解析性敏感性指数（PSI）两种度量标准，在14万次生成实验中发现，不同模型的平均FSI差异超过30倍，主要由合规失败解释。固定效应回归表明，即使控制任务、模型和包装因素，可解析性仍是准确率的强预测因子。作者认为不报告包装方差和合规性的准确率统计是脆弱的，并为基准测试和结构化输出部署提供了实用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示词包装仅在格式上有所不同，却能显著改变模型评分，甚至翻转排行榜结论。这种方差未被现有评估体系充分捕捉，导致基准测试结果统计上不可靠。

Method: 在令牌受控协议下，引入FSI（准确率范围）和PSI（可解析性范围）两种互补指标。使用OpenRouter进行14万次生成实验，涵盖7个QA任务、5种包装家族、4个指令模型（7B到72B参数），并进行固定效应回归分析。

Result: 不同模型的平均FSI差异超过30倍，主要由合规失败解释。可解析性在控制任务、模型和包装后仍是准确率的强预测因子。

Conclusion: 不报告包装方差和合规性的准确率统计是脆弱的。研究为基准测试和结构化输出部署提供了实用建议。

Abstract: Prompt wrappers often differ only in formatting, yet they can change model scores enough to flip leaderboard conclusions. We study this variance under a token-controlled protocol and introduce two complementary metrics: the Format Sensitivity Index (FSI), the accuracy range induced by wrapper choice, and the Parseability Sensitivity Index (PSI), the corresponding range in answer parseability. Across 140,000 OpenRouter generations spanning 7 QA tasks, 5 wrapper families, and 4 instruct models from 7B to 72B parameters, we find that mean FSI varies by over 30x across models and is largely explained by compliance failures. A fixed-effects regression shows that parseability remains a strong predictor of accuracy even after controlling for task, model, and wrapper. We argue that reporting accuracy without wrapper variance and compliance is statistically fragile, and we give practical recommendations for both benchmarking and structured-output deployments.

</details>


### [95] [Faithful, Not Corrective: Message-Format Effects in Multi-Hop Agent Relays Are Tier-Dependent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09678)
*Zayx Shawn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过受控的中继测试平台，系统比较了五种信息格式（自由NL、精准NL、JSON、三元组、键值对）在多跳LLM代理通信中的表现。发现格式效应依赖于中继能力：强中继几乎无损，弱中继下格式间召回差异扩大8.7倍，由编码代价和漂移抵抗力两种机制驱动。格式选择应遵循流水线中最弱的中继环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献在信息格式对LLM代理通信的影响上存在分歧：格式优化工作认为结构化消息可降低成本且不损准确性，而格式约束工作发现施加结构会降低生成质量。同时，两者均未测量消息在多跳传输中的副本保真度（而非单次生成），因此需要系统研究不同格式在多跳中继中的表现。

Method: 设计受控中继测试平台：12个程序化生成的原子事实简报在五种格式（自由NL、精准指令NL、JSON、三元组、键值对）下逐跳重新编码，共6跳。使用固定强评分器对比程序化真实值进行评分，涵盖两种中继能力等级、认知负载条件和配对分叉错误注入。

Result: (i)强中继在忠实中继指令下近乎无损，电话游戏坍塌未出现；认知负载不改变格式级保真度（±1.8分内），但生成成本增加24-53%。(ii)弱中继（1.5B）下跨格式6跳召回差异扩大8.7倍（从2.3到20.5分），由编码代价和JSON固定键模式的漂移抵抗力驱动，格式排名在传输中翻转。(iii)分叉错误注入中，错误值在83-100%的链中持续到最终跳，与真实值的保持率高度吻合，对相邻事实无附带损害。

Conclusion: 结构化格式提供的是忠实、错误定位的信道，而非纠错码；格式选择应遵循流水线中最弱的中继环节。

Abstract: When LLM agents hand off information to one another, does the message format matter? Two literatures disagree: format-optimization work reports that structured messages cut cost without hurting accuracy, while format-restriction work finds that imposing structure degrades generation -- and neither measures what happens when a message traverses multiple hops, where copy fidelity, not one-shot generation, dominates. We introduce a controlled relay testbed: briefs of twelve programmatically generated atomic facts are re-encoded hop-by-hop in five formats (free NL, precision-instructed NL, JSON, triples, key-value) over six hops, scored by a fixed strong grader against programmatic ground truth, across two relay-capability tiers, a cognitive-load condition, and a paired-fork error injection. We find that message-format effects are tier-dependent. (i) Under faithful-relay instructions a strong relay is nearly lossless -- the documented "telephone-game" collapse does not occur -- and adding per-hop cognitive load leaves format-level fidelity unchanged (within +/-1.8 points) while raising generation cost by 24-53%. (ii) Under a weak (1.5B) relay the across-format spread of six-hop recall grows by a factor of 8.7 (from 2.3 to 20.5 points), driven by two opposing mechanisms -- an encoding toll paid by the rigid formats and drift resistance specific to the fixed-key JSON schema -- that flip the format ranking in transit. (iii) In a paired-fork injection, an injected wrong value, once present, persists to the final hop in 83-100% of chains in every format, closely matching each format's retention of the true value, with no detectable collateral damage to neighboring facts. Structure buys a faithful, error-localizing channel -- not an error-correcting code -- and format choice should follow the weakest relay in the pipeline.

</details>


### [96] [Boltzmann MapReduce: A Partition-Function Reduce for Forkable Sandboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09689)
*Yossi Eliaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在局部渐进正态性（LAN）框架下，证明了工作者在大小为n的数据块上输出的置信密度服从吉布斯-玻尔兹曼分布，其逆温度等于样本大小。该结论在高斯/线性情形下精确成立，在一般情形下为一阶近似。由此导出三个推论：不重叠数据块的玻尔兹曼因子独立，因此MapReduce的reduce操作本质上就是配分函数Z，其众数对应精度加权（逆方差）池化；频率派一致性对应零温极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立置信密度与统计物理中吉布斯-玻尔兹曼分布的严格对应关系，从而用热力学/统计力学视角重新理解分布式统计推断中的核心操作（如MapReduce的reduce阶段和频率派一致性）。

Method: 基于局部渐进正态性（LAN）理论，推导置信密度的渐近形式，识别其为吉布斯-玻尔兹曼测度，逆温度β等于样本量n；利用独立数据块的似然可乘性证明玻尔兹曼因子独立性；将MapReduce的reduce阶段重新解释为配分函数计算。

Result: 1) 置信密度= exp{-nE(θ)}的吉布斯分布；2) 不重叠数据块贡献独立的玻尔兹曼因子，reduce操作对应配分函数Z=∫Πh_k dθ；3) 众数等价于精度加权（逆方差）池化；4) 频率派一致性对应零温极限T=1/n→0。

Conclusion: 该发现揭示了统计推断与统计力学之间的深层联系，为分布式计算中的信息融合、不确定性量化以及贝叶斯-频率派一致性提供了统一的热力学解释框架。

Abstract: To leading order under local asymptotic normality (LAN), the confidence density a worker emits over a chunk of size $n$ is a Gibbs--Boltzmann measure $\exp\{-βE(θ)\}$ whose inverse temperature is the sample size, $β=n$. Three consequences are exact in the Gaussian/linear case and first-order otherwise: disjoint chunks carry independent Boltzmann factors, so the MapReduce \emph{reduce}, read literally, is a partition function $Z=\int\prod_k h_k\,dθ$ whose mode is precision-weighted (inverse-variance) pooling; frequentist consistency is the zero-temperature limit $T=1/n\to0$

</details>


### [97] [Interpreting Latent CoT Reasoning as Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09698)
*Sabari Iyyappan Duraipandian,Shreya Sanjay Boyane,Manju Nagesh,Jerome Francis,Archana Vaidheeswaran,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对隐式思维链（latent CoT）推理的不可解释性问题，引入动力系统分析方法（步幅变化、方向一致性、Lyapunov敏感度等定量指标及UMAP、DMD/PHATE定性投影），揭示CODI和COCONUT分别表现为稳定吸引子与不稳定扩张系统两种不同的动力学行为，SIM-CoT监督可收紧行为但不改变底层动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式推理方法（如CODI、COCONUT）在隐空间中同时维护多条叠加候选轨迹，缺乏显式CoT的单步透明可解释性；现有机械可解释性方法仅能揭示压缩、捷径和叠加现象，无法解释推理如何在隐层步骤间演化，亟需一个系统框架来刻画隐式CoT的推理动力学。

Method: 将隐式token序列建模为表示空间中的轨迹，应用动力系统分析：定量指标包括步间变化（step-to-step change）、方向一致性（direction consistency）、Lyapunov敏感度；定性投影包括UMAP和DMD/PHATE降维可视化。对CODI和COCONUT在有无SIM-CoT监督下的动力学行为进行对比分析。

Result: 隐式CoT表现出结构化的非随机动力学，存在两种不同的稳定性类别：CODI为稳定吸引子（stable attractor）行为，COCONUT为不稳定扩张系统（unstable expanding system）行为；SIM-CoT监督能收紧两类行为（使轨迹更集中），但不改变其底层动力学模式。

Conclusion: 该框架推进了隐式CoT推理动力学的可解释性，为改进隐式推理性能提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: Recent latent reasoning methods, such as CODI and COCONUT, face a fundamental interpretability problem: they maintain multiple superimposed candidate traces in the hidden space at each step, unlike explicit- CoT, which follows a single transparent reasoning trace. Existing mechanistic methods show compression, shortcuts, and superposition without explaining how reasoning evolves across latent steps. To address this gap, we model latent token sequences as trajectories in representation space and apply dynamical systems analysis to characterize the evolution of reasoning. Using quantitative measures, such as step-to-step change, direction consistency, and Lyapunov sensitivity, alongside qualitative projections, such as UMAP and DMD/PHATE, we show that latent CoT exhibits structured, non-random dynamics with two distinct stability classes. CODI behaves as a stable attractor, while COCONUT behaves as an unstable expanding system, and SIM-CoT supervision tightens both behaviors without changing the underlying dynamics. This framework advances the interpretability of latent CoT reasoning dynamics and provides actionable insights for improving latent reasoning performance. Code1 and Project page2 available online.

</details>


### [98] [YUKTI: From Natural-Language Situations to Robust, Verifiable Decisions An Uncertainty-Typed Proposition IR, Assumption-Robust Pareto Frontiers, and a Regret Certificate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09706)
*Suyash Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: YUKTI提出了一种新的自动规划问题形式化方法，用带类型命题图表示不确定性、形状先验和来源，通过分布化Pareto传递和多求解器路由生成假设鲁棒的决策前沿，在多个验证中大幅降低遗憾值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型驱动的决策管线（NL4Opt, OptiMUS, ORLM, OR-LLM-Agent）将问题转化为单一目标与点值系数后求解一次。对于分配真实预算、精力或临床注意力的决策而言，这种过度自信是失败模式：每个被对象化的数字都是一个假设，仅在猜测完全准确时最优的计划是脆弱的，只是计算的模仿。

Method: YUKTI的表示采用带类型命题图，关系携带形状先验、系数不确定性和来源。每个阶段路由到精确、非线性或进化求解器；通过分布化Pareto传递耦合格阶段；引入假设鲁棒Pareto前沿（ARPF），通过重采样假设（包括结构ε-污染）计算每个动作存活的频率（rho）。证明了rho是决策遗憾的精确因子的界，添加可审计可追溯性，并在无基准时合成了基准忠实的数据基础（SRJANA）。

Result: 在受控错误设定下，鲁棒折中相比朴素点计划将均值和尾部遗憾降低90%以上；在受监管的商业决策中，在合法动作空间内优化并以欧元定价下行风险；在包含41,188个决策的真实公共数据集上，样本外回测比记录的现状优34%，比朴素点规则优4%，同时减少了优化者的诅咒。头对头对比显示，给定正确数字的LLM和单目标优化均产生约47倍于YUKTI的保留遗憾。

Conclusion: YUKTI证明了将不确定性和假设鲁棒性纳入自动形式化阶段的价值，使LLM作为形式化工具而非求解器更为有效。在长程因果耦合下，前向传递变得不健全，需转向逆向归纳因果策略。

Abstract: Language models turn a worded situation into a numeric plan, and the dominant pipelines (NL4Opt, OptiMUS, ORLM, OR-LLM-Agent) commit to a single objective and point-valued coefficients, then solve once. For decisions that allocate real budget, effort, or clinical attention, that confidence is the failure mode: every objectified number is an assumption, and a plan optimal only if the guesses are exactly right is fragile -- mimicry of computation. YUKTI changes the target of autoformulation. Its representation is a typed-proposition graph whose relationships carry shape priors, coefficient uncertainty, and provenance. YUKTI routes each stage to an exact, nonlinear, or evolutionary solver; couples stages by a distributional Pareto hand-off; and introduces Assumption-Robust Pareto Frontiers (ARPF), resampling assumptions (including structural epsilon-contamination) to score how often each action survives (rho). We prove a bound making rho an exact factor of decision regret, add auditable traceability, and synthesize a benchmark-faithful data foundation when none exists (SRJANA). We validate three ways: under controlled misspecification the robust compromise cuts mean and tail regret by over 90% versus a naive point plan; on a regulated commercial decision we optimize inside a lawful action space and price the downside in euros; and on a real public dataset of 41,188 decisions an out-of-sample backtest beats the logged status quo by 34% and a naive point rule by 4% while reducing the optimizer's curse. The solvers are standard; we claim no benchmark-SOTA win. A head-to-head shows an LLM given the correct numbers, and single-objective optimization, both incur about 47x the held-out regret of YUKTI -- an LLM is a formulator, not a solver. Under long-range causal coupling, the forward hand-off becomes unsound, locating where it must become a backward-induction causal policy.

</details>


### [99] [GES-TSP: Graph Edge Sparsification for TSP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09708)
*Tianfeng Chen,Xianyue Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习的图边稀疏化方法（GES），用于欧几里得旅行商问题。该方法通过结合几何结构信息和组合优化技术，为不同实例自适应生成稀疏化图，在MATILDA数据集上可剪枝高达95%的边且最优解间隙保持在1%以内，在TSPLIB大规模实例上剪枝率超过99%而最优性间隙仍低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确求解大规模TSP实例计算成本高昂，传统图稀疏化方法依赖固定启发式规则，未能充分利用实例特定的结构信息，导致效率受限。

Method: 提出GES（Graph Edge Sparsification）方法，一种基于学习的稀疏化方法。结合几何结构信息（欧几里得距离等）与组合优化技术，为不同TSP实例自适应地生成稀疏化图，从而显著减小图规模。

Result: 在MATILDA数据集上可剪枝高达95%的边，同时最优解间隙保持在1%以内；在TSPLIB基准上展现出强泛化能力，大规模实例剪枝率超过99%，最优性间隙仍低于1%。

Conclusion: 所提出的GES方法能够有效利用实例结构信息进行自适应图稀疏化，在大幅降低计算成本的同时保持接近最优的解质量，并具有良好的泛化性能。

Abstract: Solving large-scale instances of the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) exactly is computationally expensive. Researchers often employ graph sparsification methods to improve computational efficiency. Traditional sparsification methods typically rely on fixed heuristics and fail to fully exploit instance-specific structural information. In this paper, we propose Graph Edge Sparsification (GES), a learning-based sparsification approach for Euclidean TSP. By incorporating geometric structural information and combinatorial optimization technology, our proposed method adaptively generates a sparsification graph for different instances, significantly reducing the graph size and accelerating the solving process. Experimental results demonstrate that our sparsification method can prune up to 95% of edges on the MATILDA dataset, while keeping the solution gap within 1% of the optimal value. Moreover, our approach exhibits strong generalization capability on the TSPLIB benchmark.In some large-scale instances, the pruning rate exceeds 99%, while the optimality gap remains below 1%.

</details>


### [100] [The Verifier is the Curriculum: Execution-Gated Self-Distillation for Cross-Family Game Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09709)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Shuning Wu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于确定性、无博弈的“严格启动”（strict-launch）过滤器的自我蒸馏方法，用于提升代码生成模型的泛化能力。在GameCraft-Bench基准上，使用14B模型进行三轮拒绝采样自我蒸馏，将干净生成率从8.8%提升至42.2%，最佳K覆盖率达到25/25（天花板）。实验通过多种控制实验证明，增益来源于验证器的精度而非数据量或优化器本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对后训练阶段使用学习型评判器对代码生成器进行优化时，模型会优化代理特征（提高分数但不改善实际产出）的问题，作者希望寻找一种确定性的、无裁判的、不可博弈的过滤器信号来替代可学习的评判器。

Method: 采用拒绝采样自我蒸馏方法，以“严格启动”（strict-launch）作为确定性过滤器——即生成的游戏项目能在无头引擎下干净启动。在GameCraft-Bench（自然语言需求→完整Godot项目）上，用Qwen3-14B+LoRA模型进行三轮迭代：每轮用当前模型生成候选项目，通过严格启动过滤器筛选出通过的样本，再用这些样本进行自我蒸馏训练下一轮模型。

Result: 14B模型在严格启动过滤下，干净生成率从8.8%提升至42.2%，最佳K覆盖率从18/25提升至25/25（天花板）。每一轮增益均显著（p=0.0019, p<1e-4, p<1e-4）。对比实验：同等数据量的金标重复控制组反而退步至5.6%；将过滤器换为宽松的BUILD检查（通过率99.9%）则增益完全消失；头无执行接地性指标随轮次单调上升。

Conclusion: 验证器（verifier）即课程——模型学到的是验证器所认证的内容。确定性、不可博弈的验证信号能够指导模型学习真正功能性的能力，而非代理特征。游戏生成是一个可验证的试验床，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Post-training a code generator against a learned judge can optimize proxy features that raise the score without improving the artifact. We study the opposite signal: a deterministic, judge-free, ungameable filter -- whether a generated project launches cleanly under a headless engine (strict-launch). Under this gate, rejection-sampling self-distillation compounds out-of-family generalization. On GameCraft-Bench (mapping a natural-language brief to a complete Godot project), a 14B model (Qwen3-14B+LoRA) distilled under strict-launch raises clean generation on four unseen game families from 8.8% to 42.2% per-candidate and best-of-K coverage from 18/25 to 25/25 (the gold ceiling) over three rounds, each a significant gain (p=0.0019, p<1e-4, p<1e-4). The gain is not from merely adding data: an exactly-matched gold-duplication control regresses below the base model (5.6% vs. 8.8%, p=0.019), while a count-matched decomposition splits the round-1-to-2 jump into comparable quality (+8.8pp) and quantity (+8.5pp) channels. Most directly, rerunning the loop with only the filter swapped -- the lenient BUILD check, which passes 99.9% of generations, in place of the launch gate -- erases the gain entirely (back to base, p=1e-3 vs. the launch-gated round), isolating verifier precision rather than the optimizer. A second ungameable signal, headless execution grounding, rises monotonically across rounds and yields far more grounded candidates than gold-duplication at a matched budget (16 vs. 5), confirming the gains are functional, not launch-but-empty. Game generation is a verifiable testbed for one lesson: the verifier is the curriculum -- what it certifies is what the model learns.

</details>


### [101] [Closed-Loop Control with Rule-Aligned Small Language Models and Multi-Agent Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09713)
*Yuchen Wang,Javal Vyas,Tong Liu,Mehmet Mercangoz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究使用小型语言模型（Qwen2.5-1.5B）结合GRPO对齐和验证器引导的纠错循环，在边缘端实现自然语言到工业控制策略的生成，达到91.5%的动作对齐准确率和3.84秒平均推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现从自然语言需求规格自动创建和重构控制策略是自主工业操作的关键，但现有大云模型在边缘闭环使用中存在推理延迟高、不透明或数据敏感等问题，亟需更轻量高效的解决方案。

Method: 采用Qwen2.5-1.5B小型语言模型，通过GRPO进行对齐训练，构建包含动作智能体、符号/数字孪生验证层和重新提示智能体的迭代纠错循环架构，在随机热控制仿真中进行评估。

Result: 在30组实验（每组500步）中，框架达到91.5%平均动作对齐准确率（范围86.3%-100%），平均推理延迟3.84秒；在符号重映射下保持95%的范围内率。

Conclusion: SLM+验证器架构是实现边缘端可重构自主控制的一条实用路径，在推理速度和控制可靠性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: A key step toward autonomous industrial operation is the ability to create and reconfigure control policies from natural-language requirement specifications, with minimal or no manual redesign. In this setting, policy generation by AI agents can be a credible path when paired with a plant-aware validator (e.g., a digital twin) that can check generated candidate actions before execution. However, practical deployment is constrained by inference latency and compute footprint: large cloud-based models are often too slow, opaque, or data-sensitive for edge closed-loop use. This work investigates whether a compact Small Language Model (SLM) can be retrained for control reasoning and embedded in a validator-guided correction loop. We use a Qwen2.5-1.5B model aligned via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), combined with (i) an action agent, (ii) a symbolic/digital-twin-style validation layer, and (iii) a reprompting agent that iteratively steers outputs toward valid actions. In randomized thermal-control simulations (30 experiments with 500 steps each), the framework achieves 91.5% average action-alignment accuracy (86.3%--100% across cases) at 3.84\,s mean inference latency. Under symbolic re-mapping, it maintains a 95% in-range rate, indicating robust physical regulation despite reduced token-level agreement. These results support SLM+validator architectures as a practical path toward reconfigurable autonomous control at the edge.

</details>


### [102] [Feedback-Coupled Memory Systems in Continuous Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09714)
*Stefano Grassi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了反馈耦合记忆系统（FCMS）架构，通过机制智能（MBI）定义代理更新算子 $f_i$，通过耦合记忆图过程（CMGP）定义环境更新算子 $\Psi$，建立了连续时间FCMS的Lyapunov全局耗散性，证明了记忆耗散必须超过反馈增益的普适组织原则，并通过仿真验证了稳定性阈值和自强化协调级联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始FCMS框架中两个关键算子（代理更新算子 $f_i$ 和环境更新算子 $\Psi$）被公理化为未定义，限制了框架的可计算性和物理可实现性。本文旨在为这两个算子提供具体的计算定义，从而将FCMS从抽象形式化架构转化为可实例化的连续时间动力系统。

Method: 采用 Mechanism-Based Intelligence (MBI) 定义 $f_i$，实现代理通过去中心化价格机制和经济原理进行局部更新；采用 Coupled Memory Graph Process (CMGP) 定义 $\Psi$，将环境视为物理基质，以非马尔可夫方式相干记录和响应轨迹历史。推导连续时间FCMS的Lyapunov全局耗散性条件 $4\beta^2 < 2\eta\mu\gamma^2$，并与离散FCMS条件 $4\eta\beta^2 < \gamma$ 及CMGP物理分岔阈值 $\alpha_c = 1/K$ 进行统一。

Result: 建立了连续时间FCMS的Lyapunov全局耗散性，推导出可计算稳定性阈值 $4\beta^2 < 2\eta\mu\gamma^2$，该条件推广了离散FCMS和CMGP的稳定性条件，证实记忆耗散超过反馈增益是普适组织原则。$N=2$ 数值仿真和 $N=10^6$ 平均场验证确认了稳定性阈值以及违反该阈值时出现的自强化协调级联现象。

Conclusion: 记忆耗散必须超过反馈增益是跨离散和连续时间FCMS框架的普适组织原则。本文为FCMS提供了完整的可计算定义和物理可实现性基础，建立的稳定性条件统一了离散FCMS和CMGP的理论结果。

Abstract: The Feedback-Coupled Memory Systems (FCMS) architecture formalizes closed-loop coordination through four abstract operators, two of which - the agent update operator $f_i$ and the environmental update operator $Ψ$ - are left axiomatically undefined in the original framework. To address this, $f_i$ is defined by Mechanism-Based Intelligence (MBI), where agents update locally through a decentralized price mechanism and economic principles, and $Ψ$ is defined by the Coupled Memory Graph Process (CMGP), a non-Markovian framework where the environment is treated as a physical substrate that records and responds to trajectory history coherently without external forcing. The resulting continuous-time FCMS instantiation achieves Lyapunov global dissipativity governed by the computable threshold $4β^2 < 2ημγ^2$. This generalizes both the discrete FCMS stability condition $4ηβ^2 < γ$ and CMGP's physical bifurcation threshold $α_c = 1/K$, confirming that memory dissipation must outpace feedback gain as a universal organizing principle. Numerical simulation with $N=2$ agents and mean-field validation at $N=10^6$ confirm the stability threshold and the self-reinforcing coordination cascade that emerges when it is violated.

</details>


### [103] [A Symbolic Neural CPU for Quantization-Simulated Writeback and Interpretable Program Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10021)
*Jose Luis Lima de Jesus Silva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种迹监督符号神经CPU架构，通过分解学习架构（循环控制、显式操作路由器、固定微分算术逻辑单元库、目标掩码寄存器写回、完整轨迹监督和定点回放）使神经网络的执行状态转换过程完全可见。在16宽度基准上，非量化执行器完美复现参考执行，8比特量化模拟执行器在1000指令程序上保持符号操作路径。通过比较多种控制器和消融实验，证明操作门监督对可检查执行路径的必要性，并扩展了值记忆、混合自适应LIF控制器、候选约束符号控制等接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络可以学习算法输入输出映射，但仅凭最终正确答案不足以信任学习到的执行器，因为产生最终答案的状态转换过程通常是隐藏的。为了使这些状态转换可见，需要设计一个能暴露每步操作、源和目标寄存器、寄存器轨迹、内存信号和写回语义的可解释执行架构。

Method: 提出迹监督符号神经CPU，一种分解学习执行架构，包含：循环控制、显式操作路由器（路由至固定可微算术逻辑单元库）、目标掩码寄存器写回、完整轨迹监督（监督每步操作选择）、匹配定点回放。比较了循环、Transformer、时序卷积、时序图启发和状态空间五种控制器。扩展了值存储器、混合自适应LIF控制器、通过行为克隆和演员-评论家强化学习训练的候选约束符号控制，以及RV32I基础整数语义桥接。

Result: 非量化执行器在16宽度基准上精确复现参考执行；8比特量化模拟执行器在1000指令程序上保持符号操作路径；匹配定点回放消除残余数值漂移，证明漂移源于连续与低精度参考语义不匹配而非执行失败；消融实验表明操作门监督对可检查执行路径是必要的；隐操作码内存压力任务暴露了延迟状态使用和时间绑定的局限性。

Conclusion: 建立了迹可验证框架，用于可解释、低精度和可控的神经执行，使神经网络的内部执行状态转换过程完全透明可检查。

Abstract: Neural networks can learn algorithmic input-output mappings, but trusting a learned executor requires more than a correct final answer because the state transitions that produce it are usually hidden. To make those transitions visible, we introduce a trace-supervised symbolic neural CPU, a factorized learned execution architecture that combines recurrent control, an explicit operation router over a fixed differentiable arithmetic-logic unit bank, destination-masked register writeback, complete trajectory supervision and matched fixed-point replay. The model exposes the selected operation, source and destination registers, register trajectory, memory signals and writeback semantics at every step. On the principal 16-wide benchmark, the non-quantized executor reproduces reference execution exactly, while the eight-bit quantization-simulated executor preserves the symbolic operation path through programs of 1,000 instructions. When the same execution is evaluated against a matched fixed-point replay, the residual numerical drift disappears, showing that it comes from a mismatch between continuous and low-precision reference semantics rather than from execution failure. We compare recurrent, Transformer, temporal-convolution, temporal graph-inspired and state-space controllers, and the ablations show that operation-gate supervision is necessary for an inspectable execution path. Hidden-opcode memory-pressure tasks expose the remaining limits in delayed state use and temporal binding. We also extend the interface with ValueMemory, hybrid adaptive leaky integrate-and-fire controllers, candidate-constrained symbolic control trained through behaviour cloning and actor-critic reinforcement learning, and an RV32I base-integer semantic bridge. Together, these results establish a trace-verifiable framework for interpretable, low-precision and controllable neural execution.

</details>


### [104] [AGM-like Paraconsistent Partial Meet Abductive Expansion Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09729)
*Ulisses Franceschi Eliano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于Pagnucco的溯因扩展操作和Aliseda的分类法，提出了首个AGM风格的次协调溯因扩展操作AGMpabd，利用次协调逻辑RCbr（一种LFI）避免矛盾导致的认识论荒谬状态，并给出了其公设和传递关系偏交构造。这是系列论文的第一篇，第二篇将赋予次协调算子更重要的认识论角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AGM溯因扩展操作无法处理相互矛盾的解释性假设，会导致认识论荒谬状态（琐碎化）。本文旨在创建一种能容纳矛盾假设而不导致琐碎化的次协调溯因扩展操作。

Method: 以Pagnucco的AGM溯因扩展操作和Aliseda的溯因推理分类法为基础，采用最近创建的次协调逻辑RCbr（一种满足自外延性的LFI），构建新的AGMpabd系统，定义其公设和传递关系偏交构造。

Result: 成功构建了首个AGM风格的次协调溯因扩展操作AGMpabd，给出了相应的公设集合和传递关系偏交构造表示定理。该操作能吸收矛盾的解释性假设而不导致琐碎化。

Conclusion: 本文的AGMpabd是AGM文献中首个此类操作，但次协调否定和一致性算子尚未发挥实质的认识论作用。后续论文将在此基础上增强这些算子的角色，形成AGMcircabd系统。

Abstract: In his 1996 doctoral thesis, Maurice Pagnucco created the first AGM-like abductive expansion operation. Taking his operation as a basis, as well as a taxonomy -- inspired by Atocha Aliseda -- responsible for highlighting and formalizing the main components of abductive reasoning, the main aim of this paper is to present a new paraconsistent AGM-like abductive expansion operation -- capable of assimilating contradictory explanatory hypotheses without trivialization and the consequent absurd epistemic state -- with its postulates and its transitively relational partial meet construction. To a large extent, the formal development presented in this paper was only made possible by the recent creation of the paraconsistent logic RCbr, an LFI (Logics of Formal Inconsistencies) that establishes properties especially relevant to belief revision contexts, in particular, the ability to be self-extensional -- i.e., to satisfy the replacement property. This is the first of two papers: the paraconsistent abductive expansion operation announced here -- which is part of a new system called AGMpabd -- despite bringing many interesting features, does not assign any relevant epistemic role to the paraconsistent operators of negation and consistency. Only in a second paper will an analogous paraconsistent abductive expansion operation -- which is part of another new system, AGMcircabd -- be enhanced in this direction. Nevertheless, to the best of my knowledge, the operation developed in this paper is the first of its kind in the AGM literature.

</details>


### [105] [Coresets Before Score Sets: Evaluation-Unsupervised Prompt Subset Selection for LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09739)
*Jihan Yao,Gantavya Bhatt,Arnav Das,Peter Jin,Ke Bao,Qiaolin Yu,Khushi Bhardwaj,Chang Su,Jialei Wang,Yikai Zhu,Sugam Devare,Damon Mosk-Aoyama,Zhen Dong,Venkat Krishna Srinivasan,Yineng Zhang,Oleksii Kuchaiev,Jiantao Jiao,Banghua Zhu,Jeff Bilmes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM基准测试的核集选择问题，提出了一种无监督的评估方法，利用次模子集选择从多个基准中选取少量提示，使得模型得分和排名接近完整基准套件。在涵盖35个异构基准、18个前沿LLM和超过6.1万提示的大规模评估中，基于设施定位（FL）函数的语义嵌入方法在保持模型分数方面优于12个基线方法，且该方法在监督场景下也表现出色，计算成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评估需要大量基准测试，计算成本高昂。现有基准压缩方法要么需要模型评估结果（监督），要么粒度较粗（选择整个基准）。本文希望在不依赖模型评估结果的情况下，实现细粒度的基准核集选择，从而大幅降低评估成本。

Method: 采用次模子集选择框架，在无监督（评估-无监督）设定下，仅使用廉价的语义嵌入（prompt embeddings），设计了多种次模函数，包括行列式点过程（DPP）、次模互信息和设施定位（FL）函数，用于从多个基准中挑选提示子集。在监督设定下，同样适用FL目标进行整基准选择。

Result: 在35个异构基准、18个LLM、6.1万提示的大规模实验中，FL函数在保持模型分数方面优于12个基于分数和多样性的基线方法。在监督场景下，FL方法在MMLU和MTEB排行榜上匹配或超越最先进基线，且计算成本更低。

Conclusion: 次模性是一种强大且可靠的基准压缩工具。基于设施定位的语义嵌入选择方法在无监督和有监督设定下均表现优异，为实现低成本、高保真的LLM评估提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We study LLM benchmark coreset selection: selecting a small subset of prompts over multiple benchmarks whose induced model scores and rankings approximate those obtained from the full benchmark suite. In evaluation-unsupervised benchmark coreset selection (our approach), the selection algorithm uses no model evaluation outcomes, and operates on a fine granularity by producing subsets of prompts over multiple benchmarks rather than producing a sub-collection of entire benchmarks. We use submodular subset selection, and we develop and evaluate many different submodular functions for this purpose, including determinantal point process (DPP) based approaches, submodular mutual information functions, and facility location-based functions. On a new large-scale suite of 35 heterogeneous benchmarks spanning five different capability categories, 18 frontier LLMs, and over 61K prompts, we find that the facility location (FL) function operating exclusively on inexpensive semantic prompt embeddings preserves LLM scores better than twelve separate score-based and diversity-based baselines, across a range of coreset budgets. Moreover, we show our proposed objective is not limited to the evaluation-unsupervised regime: in the setting where only a handful of whole benchmarks must be selected and a large amount of model scores are available, the same objective matches or outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on the MMLU and MTEB leaderboards, while being substantially cheaper to compute. Together, our results suggest that submodularity, in general, is a strong and reliable tool for benchmark compression.

</details>


### [106] [A Dynamic Scene Interaction Reasoning Framework for Scene-level Lane-Change Intention and Trajectory Prediction of Multiple Interacting Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09740)
*Joshua Kofi Asamoah,Blessing Agyei Kyem,Eugene Denteh,Armstrong Aboah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出动态场景图注意力框架(DSiGAT)，同时预测交通场景中所有相关车辆的换道意图和未来轨迹，通过时间图注意力消息传递和意图引导解码器实现多车辆协调预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有换道预测方法多聚焦于单一目标车辆，而多智能体预测方法仅描述未来位置，缺乏对每辆车具体机动行为的显式信息。

Method: 将场景构建为时变交互图，车辆为节点，空间和运动学关系为边特征；使用时间图注意力消息传递捕获车辆间依赖关系和预机动线索；意图引导解码器关联预测机动与未来运动；场景级一致性目标促进多车未来兼容。

Result: 在NGSIM I-80/US-101和highD数据集上，意图预测准确率分别达90.12%和90.97%，轨迹RMSE较最强基线降低52.94%，碰撞率和联合位移误差更低。

Conclusion: DSiGAT通过显式建模场景级交互和图注意力机制，显著提升了多车辆换道意图与轨迹联合预测的性能和一致性。

Abstract: Safe motion planning in advanced driver-assistance systems and autonomous vehicles requires an accurate understanding of how the surrounding traffic scene is likely to evolve. However, many existing lane-change prediction methods remain centered on a single target vehicle, while multi-agent forecasting approaches often describe scene evolution only through future positions and provide limited explicit information about the maneuver associated with each vehicle. This study proposes a dynamic scene graph attention framework that predicts the lane-change intention and future trajectory of every relevant vehicle within a local traffic scene. The scene is represented as a time-varying interaction graph in which vehicles are modeled as nodes and their spatial and kinematic relationships are encoded through explicit edge features. Temporal graph-attention message passing captures evolving inter-vehicle dependencies and pre-maneuver cues, while an intention-guided decoder links each predicted maneuver to its corresponding future motion. A scene-level consistency objective further encourages compatible multi-vehicle futures. Experiments on the NGSIM I-80, NGSIM US-101, and highD datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over competing baselines. DSiGAT achieves intention prediction accuracies of 90.12% and 90.97% on NGSIM I-80 and US-101, respectively, and reduces trajectory RMSE by up to 52.94% relative to the strongest baseline. It also produces lower inter-agent collision rates and joint displacement errors, indicating more coherent scene-level predictions. Ablation, sensitivity, robustness, and qualitative analyses further validate the contribution of the proposed components and the effectiveness of the scene-focused formulation.

</details>


### [107] [Scaffolding the Strategist: Architecture-Dependent Reasoning Interventions in Hotelling Spatial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09743)
*Pratyush Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了结构化推理干预对大型语言模型战略经济推理的影响，发现干预效果与模型架构存在显著交互作用：承诺式脚手架提升标准模型但降低推理模型表现，原则分离则相反；对抗性压力测试对两种模型均有损害，且对推理模型伤害更大（2.6倍）。同时发现声明性-程序性知识差距，原则分离可完全弥合推理模型的这一差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究结构化推理干预是否能改善大语言模型的战略经济推理能力，以及这种效应是否依赖于模型架构。

Method: 使用Hotelling线性城市模型作为诊断工具，评估GPT-4.1-mini（标准指令跟随模型）和GPT-5-mini（推理优化模型）在五种条件（无脚手架基线+四种推理干预）下的表现，涵盖8个问题（演绎与溯因推理）、3种提示框架、3次重复，共720个独立判断的响应。

Result: 发现脚手架类型与模型架构之间存在统计显著的交叉交互作用（t(7)=4.79, p=0.002, d=1.69）：承诺式脚手架提升标准模型（+0.21）但降低推理模型（-0.63），原则分离则相反（-0.40 vs +0.31）。对抗性压力测试对两种模型均有损害，推理模型劣化程度是标准模型的2.6倍（-1.47 vs -0.57, p=0.038）。存在声明性-程序性知识差距，原则分离可完全弥合推理模型此差距。

Conclusion: 推理干预的效果高度依赖于模型架构，不存在通用的最佳干预策略；不同模型需要针对其架构特点定制化设计的推理脚手架；声明性-程序性知识差距的存在表明模型"知道"正确策略与"执行"正确策略之间存在鸿沟。

Abstract: We investigate whether structured reasoning interventions improve the strategic economic reasoning of large language models, and whether their effects depend on model architecture. Using Hotelling's linear city model as a diagnostic vehicle, we evaluate GPT-4.1-mini (a standard instruction-following model) and GPT-5-mini (a reasoning-optimized model) under five conditions - an unscaffolded baseline and four reasoning interventions - across eight questions spanning deductive and abductive reasoning, three prompt framings, and three repetitions per condition, yielding 720 individually judged responses. We find a statistically significant crossover interaction between scaffolding type and model architecture ($t(7) = 4.79$, $p = 0.002$, $d = 1.69$): commitment scaffolding improves the standard model ($+0.21$) while degrading the reasoning model ($-0.63$), and principled separation shows the opposite pattern ($-0.40$ vs. $+0.31$). Both crossovers are individually significant (commitment: $p = 0.040$; separation: $p = 0.002$) and hold across all eight questions with 7/8 directional consistency. Adversarial stress-testing harms both models, with $2.6\times$ greater degradation for the reasoning model ($-1.47$ vs. $-0.57$; $p = 0.038$), and the damage correlates negatively with baseline difficulty ($R^2 = 0.36$, $p = 0.014$). We further document a persistent declarative-procedural gap in which both models identify correct strategies at rates far exceeding their ability to execute them; separation fully closes this gap for the reasoning model while no intervention helps the standard model.

</details>


### [108] [A Theory of Least Autonomy in AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09744)
*Christophe Parisel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"最小自治"原则作为"最小权限"原则在智能体AI系统中的泛化，并建立了包含组合爆炸半径、有向智能体影响图和共谋谓词的正式理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的最小权限原则仅关注身份持有权限的静态控制，无法应对智能体AI系统中智能体可以组合、批准和放大权限的动态行为，因此需要一种新的形式化理论来刻画和约束智能体间的交互影响。

Method: 1) 定义组合爆炸半径 d(a,b)，结合超度量树与格值机密性、完整性和控制上下文标签，衡量企业层级中动作间的结构分离程度；2) 定义有向智能体影响图 G(θ)，包含基于共享资源写后读或双向A2A通信的边，以及基于策略阈值 θ 的影响势条件；3) 定义共谋谓词，通过图可达性检测授权组合、决策操纵和跨域能力组合。

Result: 构建了一个形式化理论体系，包含爆破半径度量、影响图校准工具（目录-半径配置文件）和共谋检测机制，能够量化评估和审计智能体AI系统中的自治程度与潜在风险。

Conclusion: 最小权限原则不足以应对智能体AI系统的安全需求，所提出的最小自治原则及配套形式化工具为设计、部署和审计安全可控的智能体系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Least privilege, the principle that an identity should hold only the permissions strictly required for its task, has been a foundational primitive of access control for decades. We argue that this principle is insufficient for agentic AI systems, which do not merely hold permissions but can combine, approve, and amplify them across workflows and system boundaries.
  We propose least autonomy as an appropriate generalization and develop a formal theory. First, we define a compositional blast radius d(a,b) that measures structural separation between actions in an enterprise hierarchy, combining an ultrametric tree with lattice-valued confidentiality, integrity, and control-context labels. Second, we define a directed agent influence graph G(theta).
