### [211] [FlashAccel: Leveraging High-Bandwidth Flash for High-Throughput LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10186)
*Xinyu Wang,Yalong Xue,Xiaotian Sun,Xiaoyu Zhang,Chunmeng Dou,Xueqi Li,Xiaoming Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: FlashAccel 是一个协同设计的系统，通过将高带宽闪存 (HBF) 集成到 GPU 中，解决大语言模型推理时 HBM 容量不足的问题。该系统通过架构优化降低 HBF 的高访问延迟、通过专用数据布局提升带宽利用率，并引入 HBF 感知的存储管理层和编程模型来协调异构内存资源。实验表明，集成六个 HBF 堆叠后，在 100ms 延迟约束下，每 GPU 吞吐量平均提升 2.54 倍，能效提升 1.93 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理越来越受限于 GPU 中高带宽内存 (HBM) 的容量，因为模型权重和 KV 缓存快速增长。HBF 虽提供更高容量和相近带宽，但其高访问延迟、低带宽利用率以及缺乏对异构资源管理的支持，使其难以直接集成到 GPU 中用于 LLM 推理。

Method: FlashAccel 通过三个层面实现高效 LLM 推理：(1) 架构支持：将 HBF 集成到基于 HBM 的 GPU 中，设计架构级优化来缓解 HBF 的高访问延迟；(2) 数据布局：针对模型权重和 KV 缓存设计专门的数据布局，提升 HBF 的带宽利用率；(3) 系统级协调：引入 HBF 感知的存储管理层和编程模型，组织 HBF 中的持久化数据并协调异构内存资源。

Result: 在 100ms 延迟约束下，集成六个 HBF 堆叠后，FlashAccel 相比纯 HBM GPU，每 GPU 吞吐量平均提升 2.54 倍，能效提升 1.93 倍。

Conclusion: HBF 是一种有前景的替代方案，可有效缓解 LLM 推理中的 HBM 容量瓶颈。FlashAccel 通过架构、数据布局和系统层面的协同设计，成功将 HBF 集成到 GPU 中，实现了显著的吞吐量和能效提升。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference is increasingly limited by the capacity of High-Bandwidth Memory (HBM) in GPUs, as model weights and KV cache grow rapidly. High-Bandwidth Flash (HBF) provides higher capacity than HBM while retaining comparable bandwidth, making it a promising substrate for capacity-constrained LLM inference. However, its inherently high access latency, low bandwidth utilization, and lack of support for heterogeneous resource management make it difficult to integrate HBF into GPUs for LLM inference. We present FlashAccel, a co-designed system that enables efficient LLM inference using HBF. FlashAccel integrates HBF into HBM-based GPUs, providing architectural support to mitigate access latency. It improves bandwidth utilization through specialized data layouts for both model weights and KV cache, and introduces an HBF-aware storage management layer together with a programming model to organize persistent data in HBF and coordinate heterogeneous memory resources at the system level. Experimental results demonstrate that integrating six HBF stacks into the GPU enables FlashAccel to deliver an average improvement of 2.54$\times$ and 1.93$\times$ in throughput per GPU and energy efficiency over the HBM-only GPU under 100ms latency constraint, respectively.

</details>


### [212] [When Fuzzing Meets Understanding: LLM-Driven Semantic Test Generation for RTL Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10340)
*Kun Wang,Cangyuan Li,Kaiyan Chang,Siyang Cai,Yinhe Han,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ChipFuzzer是一种利用大语言模型语义推理能力的硬件模糊测试框架，通过覆盖引导和缺陷引导两阶段工作流，在三个开源CPU设计上将条件覆盖率平均提升5.8个百分点，缺陷检测率提升21.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代芯片复杂度日益增长，硬件验证面临巨大挑战。现有硬件模糊测试主要依赖启发式策略，缺乏对被测试设计内部逻辑和语义行为的推理能力，难以实现高覆盖率和暴露边界缺陷。

Method: 提出ChipFuzzer框架，采用双阶段工作流：1) 覆盖引导阶段：通过控制流相似性与差异分析，引导LLM生成测试用例以提升覆盖率；2) 缺陷引导阶段：利用历史缺陷数据识别易出错的代码区域，并优先为这些区域生成测试用例以提升缺陷发现效率。

Result: 在三个开源CPU设计上，相比最强基线方法，ChipFuzzer将平均条件覆盖率提升5.8个百分点，缺陷检测率提升21.1个百分点。

Conclusion: ChipFuzzer通过LLM的语义推理能力有效提升了硬件模糊测试的覆盖率和缺陷发现效率，验证了LLM在硬件验证领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: The growing complexity of modern chips poses significant challenges to hardware verification. In recent years, coverage-guided fuzzing has emerged as a promising approach for improving verification efficiency. However, existing hardware fuzzers still struggle to achieve high coverage and expose corner-case bugs, as they predominantly rely on heuristic strategies with limited ability to reason about the internal logic and semantic behavior of the design under test (DUT). In this work, we propose ChipFuzzer, a hardware fuzzing framework that leverages the semantic reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) to improve fuzzing effectiveness. ChipFuzzer adopts a dual-stage workflow comprising a Coverage-Guided stage and a Bug-Guided stage. In the Coverage-Guided stage, ChipFuzzer employs control-flow similarity and discrepancy analysis to guide LLM-driven testcase generation, thereby improving coverage. In the Bug-Guided stage, ChipFuzzer leverages historical bug data to identify bug-prone code regions and prioritize testcase generation for those regions, thus enhancing bug discovery efficiency. Experimental results on three open-source CPU designs show that ChipFuzzer improves average condition coverage by 5.8 percentage points and bug detection rate by 21.1 percentage points over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [213] [Dynamic Rowhammer Threshold Management:Temperature-Aware Threshold Degradation for In-DRAM Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10392)
*Aziz Alajmi,Hoeseok Yang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种动态Rowhammer阈值管理(Dynamic Rowhammer Threshold Management)运行时层，根据温度动态调整DRAM的Rowhammer防御阈值，解决了制造时固定阈值与运行时温度变化导致的防御失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRAM Rowhammer防御机制在制造时固定了缓解阈值(Rowhammer Threshold, TRHD)，但实际TRHD会随运行时温度变化，导致高温下防御失效或低温下过度限制性能。

Method: 提出一种防御无关的运行时层，通过线性温度模型(linear-T)结合VRD动机的防护带(guardband g)在每个epoch从观测温度重新计算阈值，并映射到SALT-C、PRAC、TRR等具体防御的阈值参数上。同时引入一个解耦预言机，通过δ~N(1, σ)缩放每个DIMM的物理TRHD来破坏模型自洽性。

Result: 该运行时层将PRAC在85°C下的72次陈旧性违规降至0；在σ=0.10时，调整防护带g使PRAC违规从38.4(g=1.0)降至9.6(g=0.9)；SALT-C从10次名义静态违规降至2次(动态)再到0次(引导启动)，延迟≤5.1%；TRR受容量限制，该层作为诊断工具。

Conclusion: 动态温度感知的阈值管理能有效消除因温度变化导致的Rowhammer防御违规，同时保持较低的延迟开销，可作为现有防御机制的通用增强层。

Abstract: In-DRAM Rowhammer defenses pin the mitigation threshold at manufacture time, yet the true Rowhammer Threshold (TRHD) varies with runtime temperature. We propose \emph{Dynamic Rowhammer Threshold Management}, a defense-agnostic runtime layer that re-sources each defense's threshold from the observed temperature once per epoch via a linear-$T$ model with a VRD-motivated guardband $g$, projecting the result onto SALT-C, PRAC, and TRR through each defense's threshold parameter. A decoupled oracle that scales physical TRHD per-DIMM by $δ\sim \mathrm{N}(1, σ)$ breaks model self-consistency. The layer drives PRAC's 72 staleness breaches at 85$^\circ$C to zero; at $σ{=}0.10$, sweeping $g$ collapses PRAC breaches from 38.4 ($g{=}1.0$) to 9.6 ($g{=}0.9$). SALT-C drops from 10 nominal-static breaches to 2 (Dynamic) to 0 (bootstrap), at $\leq$5.1\% latency. TRR is capacity-limited; the layer acts as a diagnostic.

</details>


### [214] [Soft-Error Characterization and Hardening Trade-offs in Static PCHB Asynchronous Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10788)
*Ramya Karri,Srija Rasoori,Ashiq A. Sakib*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了静态PCHB异步数字电路的软错误特性，通过晶体管级故障注入框架提取关键电荷，识别脆弱节点，并评估四种加固策略在延时、能量和面积上的开销权衡，最终给出面向特定架构的加固设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCHB异步数字电路在恶劣环境（如高辐射空间）下具有应用潜力，但其软错误（单粒子效应）特性尚缺乏系统性的研究，因此有必要对其软错误敏感度进行深入表征，并为加固设计提供量化依据。

Method: 开发了可控的晶体管级故障注入框架，提取节点极性相关的临界电荷；通过大量仿真识别脆弱节点；针对五种代表性PCHB单元，实现并评估了四种加固策略：双端施密特触发器、单端施密特触发器、传输门加固和基于复制的冗余。

Result: 获得了各PCHB单元内部节点的极性相关临界电荷数据；识别出脆弱节点；给出了四种加固策略在延时、能量和面积上的全面开销对比结果。

Conclusion: 提出了面向特定架构的静态PCHB电路软错误加固设计指南，实现了鲁棒性与开销之间的权衡优化。

Abstract: Pre-Charge Half Buffer (PCHB) is a promising asynchronous digital design paradigm for harsh-environment operation; however, its soft-error characteristics remain largely unexplored. This paper presents a systematic soft-error characterization and hardening trade-off analysis for static PCHB circuits. A controlled transistor-level fault-injection framework is developed to extract polarity-dependent critical charge at internal nodes. Vulnerability nodes are identified based on extensive simulation. Four mitigation strategies, double-sided Schmitt trigger, single-sided Schmitt trigger, transmission-gate reinforcement, and duplication-based redundancy, are implemented and evaluated across five representative PCHB cells. Comprehensive resilience-overhead comparisons in delay, energy, and area are reported, leading to architecture-specific hardening guidelines for robust static PCHB design.

</details>


### [215] [Edge Physical AI Deployment of Vision Transformers on Heterogeneous Edge GPU Targeting Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10942)
*Ashiyana Abdul Majeed,Mahmoud Meribout,Neethu Joseph,Abel Kidane Haile,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出H-FraDS，一种面向NVIDIA边缘GPU的硬件感知帧调度方法，通过将Transformer推理任务在GPU和双DLA核心间按固定比例调度，并适配不兼容算子（张量重塑、ERF近似tanh、LayerNorm替换为tanh），在保持92% F1分数（仅降2%）的同时，实现2.36倍加速（125.93 FPS）和4.0 FPS/W能效，满足30 FPS实时要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI系统（如自动驾驶）需基于Transformer的感知模型满足边缘延迟和能耗约束，但异构边缘GPU部署受限于硬件引擎利用率不足和加速器不兼容算子，导致执行碎片化、每瓦吞吐量低。

Method: H-FraDS：硬件感知的帧调度方法，按固定比例将帧路由至GPU和双DLA核心；通过张量重塑、ERF近似tanh、LayerNorm替换为bounded tanh适配DLA；利用OFA进行光流估计。

Result: Swin Transformer自动驾驶感知任务：Balanced Dispatch (1:2) 达125.93 FPS，相较纯DLA加速2.36倍；能效4.0 FPS/W；DLA延迟约24 ms，满足30 FPS实时；GPU-DLA-OFA方案实现2.02倍DLA吞吐加速；模型F1分数92%（仅降2%）。

Conclusion: H-FraDS有效提升了边缘GPU上Transformer推理的硬件利用率与每瓦吞吐量，首次联合解决GPU/DLA/OFA异构调度问题，在精度损失极小的情况下实现显著加速和能效提升。

Abstract: Physical AI systems, such as autonomous vehicles and intelligent machines, require transformer-based perception models that satisfy stringent edge latency and energy constraints. However, heterogeneous edge-GPU deployment remains limited by underutilized hardware engines and accelerator-incompatible operators, causing fragmented execution and lower throughput per watt. This paper presents Heterogeneous Frame Dispatch Scheduling (H-FraDS), a hardware-aware frame scheduling methodology for transformer inference on a recent NVIDIA edge GPU. H-FraDS routes frames across the GPU and dual deep learning accelerator (DLA) cores using fixed dispatch ratios to improve utilization under latency and power constraints. To enable scheduling, incompatible transformer components are adapted for DLA execution by reshaping tensors, approximating error function (ERF) with tanh, and replacing layer normalization with bounded tanh. The adapted model maintains a 92% F1 score, with only a 2% reduction from the original. Optical flow accelerator (OFA) is further used for inference-side optical-flow estimation. To the best of the authors' knowledge, prior work has not addressed these combined issues. Using Swin Transformer for autonomous-driving perception, H-FraDS Balanced Dispatch (1:2) achieves 125.93 FPS, a 2.36x speedup over standalone adapted-DLA execution, 4.0 FPS/W, and approximately 24 ms DLA latency, satisfying 30 FPS real-time operation; the GPU-DLA-OFA case achieves a 2.02x DLA throughput speedup.

</details>


### [216] [IRONSmith: A Visual Dataflow Design Environment for AMD Ryzen AI NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10944)
*Brock Sorenson,Samer Ali,Curt John Bansil,Aman Arora*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: IRONSmith 是首个用于 AMD Ryzen AI NPU 的可视化数据流设计环境，允许用户通过图形化界面拖拽连接 tiles、配置核函数，自动生成可执行代码，无需编写任何代码即可在 NPU 上运行 ML 推理任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NPU 在 ML 推理中具有显著的吞吐量和能效优势，但编程门槛高，需要专业框架知识。IRONSmith 旨在降低 NPU 编程门槛，让缺乏硬件专长的 ML 从业者也能便捷使用 NPU。

Method: 提供可视化画布，将 AI Engine tile 网格呈现为可连接的功能块，用户通过拖拽连线（FIFO、split/join、广播、DDR 传输）构建数据流图；从预建库分配计算核，通过属性面板配置 worker 函数；后端自动完成结构补全、导入解析和依赖管理，将可视化设计翻译为可执行的 IRON Python 代码。

Result: 在 AMD Ryzen AI NPU 上成功运行了从单 tile 向量直通、多 tile 矩阵运算到完整多层感知器（MLP）等一系列复杂度递增的 ML 设计，均通过可视化方式完成设计并成功执行。

Conclusion: IRONSmith 弥合了 ML 知识与 NPU 编程技术之间的鸿沟，为教育者、学生、ML 研究人员和工程师提供了便捷的 NPU 编程途径，扩大了对日益普及的 NPU 硬件的可及性。

Abstract: Machine learning inference increasingly relies on specialized hardware accelerators for throughput and power efficiency. Neural Processing Units (NPUs), such as the AMD Ryzen AI NPU, offer significant ML advantages over CPUs and GPUs, but programming them requires expertise in specialized frameworks. We present IRONSmith, the first visual dataflow design environment for programming AMD Ryzen AI NPUs. IRONSmith provides an interactive canvas displaying the AI Engine tile grid as visually connected blocks, allowing users to design ML dataflow applications by connecting tiles with wires representing FIFOs, split/join patterns, broadcast connections, and DDR transfers without writing any code. Compute kernels are assigned from a pre-built library, and worker functions are configured through property panels. IRONSmith's backend pipeline automatically translates the visual design into executable IRON Python, handling structural completion, import resolution, and dependency management automatically. Generated code executes directly on the AMD Ryzen AI NPU. We demonstrate IRONSmith across ML designs of increasing complexity, from a single-tile vector passthrough to multi-tile matrix operations to a complete Multi-Layer Perceptron, all designed visually and successfully executed on the AMD Ryzen AI NPU. IRONSmith serves educators, students, ML researchers, and engineers by bridging the gap between ML knowledge and NPU programming expertise, widening access to hardware that is rapidly becoming standard across consumer and enterprise devices.

</details>


### [217] [Reliable Associative Lookup in Content-Addressable Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11153)
*Fan Li,Yanan Guo,Xin Xin*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文首次探索了内容可寻址存储器（CAM）的保护码设计，提出了一种非传统编码方案以解决CAM的可靠性问题，填补了该领域的长期空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAM在高速并行查找中具有关键作用，但其基于传统存储技术（SRAM、NVM）构建，继承了底层技术的可靠性挑战。传统存储系统的保护码已被广泛研究，但CAM的保护码设计却长期未被探索，存在明显的研究空白。

Method: 提出一种非传统的编码设计方案，专门针对CAM的并行查找特性和可靠性需求进行保护码设计。

Result: 首次提出了适用于CAM的保护码设计方案，填补了该领域的研究空白。

Conclusion: 本文是CAM可靠性保护码研究的初步探索，为非传统编码在CAM中的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Content Addressable Memory (CAM) is an important memory paradigm, which performs fast search by comparing an input query against all stored entries in parallel, achieving $O(1)$ lookup complexity. CAM is typically built upon conventional memory technologies, such as SRAM and Non-Volatile Memory (NVM). Accordingly, CAM can also be subject to the reliability challenges of these underlying technologies. In traditional memory systems, protection codes play a critical role in ensuring reliability and have been extensively studied. However, protection codes for CAM have remained largely unexplored. This paper takes an initial step toward addressing this longstanding gap by introducing a non-traditional code design.

</details>


### [218] [From Tool Invocation to Source-Mechanism Exploration: Protected White-Box DSE for Open-Source EDA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11294)
*Zhiyu Zheng,Yiming Du,Ziyi Wang,Zhiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ReviewDSE是一个受保护的白盒设计空间探索框架，通过评估开放源代码EDA工具中的源级机制并记录可复用搜索知识，在OpenROAD详细布局场景下相比黑盒方法实现了1.78%的HPWL降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源EDA工具允许设计空间探索超越公开旋钮，进入阶段优化器内部的有界源级机制，但现有黑盒方法无法有效探索这些内部机制。

Method: 提出ReviewDSE框架：首先从校准设计构建方法证据和源启动分支，然后利用这些固定热启动产物初始化目标案例探索，在Teacher审查和全流程验证下进行评估，记录可复用的机制级证据。

Result: 在OpenROAD详细布局的9个目标任务上，ReviewDSE在2倍运行时门控下平均减少1.78%的HPWL，而黑盒方法仅减少0.38%；运行时感知选择在1.11倍运行时下仍保持1.68%的减少；全流程审查暴露了阶段组合性失败，源机制探索修复了硬切割行合法性失败。

Conclusion: ReviewDSE通过白盒探索源级机制而非仅调优公开旋钮，显著提升了开源EDA工具的设计空间探索效果，并能检测和修复阶段组合性问题。

Abstract: Open-source EDA tools allow design-space exploration (DSE) to move beyond public knobs and into bounded source-level mechanisms inside staged optimizers. We present ReviewDSE, a protected white-box DSE framework that explores such mechanisms for a target design. ReviewDSE evaluates complete source candidates under a protected evaluator and records reusable search knowledge as reviewed mechanism-level evidence. It first constructs method evidence and source-start branches from calibration designs, then uses these fixed warm-start products to initialize target-case exploration under Teacher review and full-flow validation. We instantiate ReviewDSE on OpenROAD detailed placement as a representative staged open-source EDA optimizer. Across nine target tasks, ReviewDSE reduces final post-DPL half-perimeter wirelength (HPWL) by 1.78\% on average under a 2$\times$ runtime gate, compared with 0.38\% for public-knob black-box DSE. A runtime-aware ReviewDSE selection retains a 1.68\% reduction at 1.11$\times$ runtime, and full-flow review exposes stage-composability failures while source-mechanism exploration repairs hard cut-row legality failures.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [219] [Energy Calculus: A Compositional Algebra of Energy in Computational Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11087)
*Mosharaf Chowdhury,Jae-Won Chung,Jeff J. Ma,Nishil Talati,Ruofan Wu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出能量演算（energy calculus），一种将能量作为一等公民的组合代数，支持对任意DAG结构计算的能量消耗进行组合式推理，并考虑了测量不确定性和上下文敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量是AI扩展的约束瓶颈，但缺乏像计算、通信和学习那样的形式化处理。现有节能方案针对特定粒度和结构，无法组合不同方法的效应，能量仍是一个事后测量的标量，缺乏通用优化框架。

Method: 提出能量演算，基于能量元素（携带能量签名，包括时间、静态/动态能量、硬件工作点、执行上下文和测量不确定性），通过三个算子（顺序、同设备并行、跨设备并行）组合签名。建立了七条公理和约简定理，支持不确定性传播，并扩展到时间-能量帕累托前沿的组合推理。

Result: 揭示了能量区别于其他计算资源的两个独特性质：（1）顺序组合仅在元素互不上下文敏感时可交换；（2）顺序组合不分配于并行组合。约简定理可在交互低于测量不确定度时恢复简单上下文无关代数。

Conclusion: 能量演算为能量感知的计算系统设计提供了形式化基础，使得不同节能策略的效果可以组合推理，每个预测都带有误差界，并支持时间-能量权衡的组合分析。

Abstract: Energy is a binding constraint for AI scaling, yet it lacks the formal treatment that computation, communication, and learning have long enjoyed. Recent systems demonstrate large energy savings, but each targets a specific granularity and structure; one cannot combine frequency scaling from one system with critical-path analysis from another and reason about their joint effect on total energy. Energy remains a monolithic scalar that is measured after the fact and optimized with point solutions that do not generalize.
  We propose energy calculus, a compositional algebra that treats energy as a first-class primitive. It builds on energy elements, units of computation whose energy we can reliably measure, each carrying an energy signature that comprises its time, its static and dynamic energy, the hardware operating point and execution context under which we measured it, and the associated measurement uncertainty. Three operators (sequential, same-device parallel, and cross-device parallel) compose signatures along the same structure as the computation itself, covering arbitrary DAG-structured executions. The algebra rests on seven axioms that capture how hardware consumes energy, and it exhibits two properties distinctive to energy among computing resources: sequential composition commutes only when elements are mutually context-insensitive, and sequential composition does not distribute over parallel composition. We also present a Reduction Theorem that recovers simple context-independent algebra whenever interactions fall below measurement uncertainty, so practitioners pay for context dependence only where the physics demands it. Uncertainty propagates through every composition, so each prediction carries an error bound. Finally, we show that the same operators extend from energy totals to time--energy Pareto frontiers, so reasoning about tradeoffs composes with the same algebra.

</details>


### [220] [Decomposing Runtime, Kernel, and Quantization Speedups via a Matched FP16 Intermediate: A Hardware-Conditioned Case Study on Four NVIDIA RTX A5000 GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11368)
*Weijia Han,Lisha Qu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对量化核函数带来的推理加速进行了归因分析，将端到端加速拆分为运行时改进与量化核函数两部分，并研究了分片、多实例部署、量化对并发用户数的影响，同时指出了采样模式和提示词池差异对结论有效性的威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化核函数的加速通常被报告为一个综合数值，混杂了权重格式、核函数和推理运行时的影响，缺少对各组成部分贡献的量化分解。本文旨在通过归因研究，分离并量化运行时改进和量化核函数各自对端到端加速的贡献。

Method: 在4块NVLink桥接的NVIDIA RTX A5000 GPU上，构建匹配的中间栈（保留快速运行时但不使用量化核函数），将全栈加速拆分为运行时部分和核函数与量化部分。通过贪心解码下的对比实验进行归因，同时利用性能分析器跟踪分片实例的通信开销，并通过NVLink与PCIe的对比实验排除链路带宽瓶颈。

Result: 全栈端到端加速达到2.58倍，其中运行时改进贡献了约三分之二（对数尺度）。三个类似模型系列中，核函数与量化部分的变动不超过1.5%。单实例跨4卡分片加速不足2倍，约80%的每token性能损失归因于协调开销；NVLink与PCIe实际带宽接近，排除了链路带宽因素。较小模型在分片与多实例间因工作负载而异，较大模型则在所有工作负载下都偏好双卡配对实例。量化将可持续并发用户数扩展至可重现的半精度内存瓶颈的约4倍。

Conclusion: 量化推理加速的主要来源是运行时优化而非量化核函数本身；跨卡分片受协调开销制约，NVLink带宽并非瓶颈；部署策略需根据模型规模和工作负载权衡；量化能显著提高并发用户承载能力。

Abstract: Reported serving speedups from quantized kernels typically bundle the weight format, the kernel, and the inference runtime into one number. We present an attribution study on four NVIDIA RTX A5000 GPUs, 24 GiB each, on a single host with NVLink-bridged pairs. A matched intermediate stack that keeps the faster runtime without the quantized kernel splits the full speedup into a runtime part and a kernel and quantization part. Under matched greedy decoding the full stack reaches $2.58\times$ end to end, with the runtime change accounting for about two thirds of that gain on a logarithmic scale; across three similar model families the kernel and quantization part moves by at most 1.5%. Sharding one instance across all four cards falls well below doubling: a profiler trace attributes about 80% of the per token shortfall to coordination, and an NVLink versus PCIe control on the same hardware shows similar realized bandwidth on both links, pointing away from link bandwidth as the cause. Whether to run one sharded instance or several independent ones depends on the workload and the model, with the ranking reversing on the larger model: the smaller model splits between sharding and multiple instances by workload, while the larger model favors two paired instances on every workload. Quantization extends sustainable concurrent users roughly four times past a reproducible half precision memory cliff. Differences in sampling mode and prompt pool between the two stacks are documented as threats to validity.

</details>


### [221] [A Note on Diameter Certification in Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09929)
*Josef Erik Sedláček*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于树结构的1-局部认证方案，可验证树的直径是否不超过d，且证书大小仅为3⌈log₂(d+1)⌉比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在局部认证模型中，一般图的直径认证需要大型证书，但树结构可以支持更高效的解决方案。作者旨在为树设计一种更紧凑的直径认证方案。

Method: 提出了一种1-局部认证（1-local certification）方案，证书大小上限为3⌈log₂(d+1)⌉比特，用于判断给定树的直径是否至多为d。

Result: 成功构造了该认证方案，证书大小仅为3⌈log₂(d+1)⌉比特，显著小于一般图所需的大型证书。

Conclusion: 该结果表明树结构在直径局部认证上可达到极低的证书复杂度，为树图上的分布式验证提供了高效方案。

Abstract: In the local certification model, certifying the diameter of general graphs requires large certificates, but trees admit more efficient solutions. In this note, we provide a $1$-local certification scheme that certifies whether the diameter of a given tree is at most $d$ using certificates of at most $3\lceil\log_2(d+1)\rceil$ bits.

</details>


### [222] [Trusted Floors Under Untrusted Learners: A Runtime Assured-SLO Guard for ML Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09992)
*Hsiu-Chi Tsai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种“学习者提议，守护者裁决”的架构，用轻量级可信守护器约束不可信学习组件的错误影响，保障租户的SLO安全。在真实2×V100和仿真环境中，该守护器在所有测试场景下将保证类请求的SLO违反率严格维持在0.0（Wilson CI上限0.0053）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代ML推理系统中，可学习的无界组件（路由器、SLO准入器）可能做出错误决策，导致租户的保证SLO被静默破坏，而底层Kubernetes层（Kueue、DRA、Gateway-API推理扩展）引入的跨层不确定性进一步加剧了这一问题。现有方法依赖学习者正确，缺乏对错误的有界防护。

Method: 将租户的保证SLO拆分为两部分：安全投影（安全投影，可控制）和聚合义务（尾延迟百分位，统计残差）。设计一个小型可信守护器包裹不可信学习者，遵循“学习者提议，守护者裁决”原则：守护器通过预留和优先级调度在运行时强制执行安全投影，无论学习准入器如何错误。

Result: 在真实2×V100上，守护器在所有学习器误校准下将保证类请求的缺失率维持在0.0（10次重复，Wilson CI上限0.0053）。在推理仿真器中，针对部署的GAIE流量控制，标签错误的路由器使保证请求缺失率从0.0变为1.0，而守护器通过真实类别预留，使标签无法破坏服务下限。

Conclusion: 守护器方法能有效约束错误学习组件对SLO的破坏，低成本确定性筛查在饱和点附近及以下都不可靠，需要进行表征以确定何时可以信任。论文在2×V100和推理仿真器上进行评估，将数据中心规模、真实模型流量控制和封闭最坏情况定理作为未来议程。

Abstract: Modern ML serving increasingly lets learned, unbounded components (routers, latency-SLO admitters, admit ladders) decide a tenant's quality of service; when one is wrong, the assured SLO can silently break, and the Kubernetes layers beneath (Kueue, DRA, the Gateway-API Inference Extension) only add cross-layer surprises. Rather than trust the learner to be right, we bound the damage a wrong one can do: a small trusted guard wraps the untrusted learner -- learned proposes, the guard disposes. A tenant's assured-SLO obligation splits into two parts with different epistemics. Its safety projection (per-request dispatch feasibility and a per-class, per-window service floor) is a controllable obligation the guard enforces at runtime, regardless of an arbitrarily-wrong learned admitter, by reservation and priority dispatch. Its aggregate obligation (a tail-latency percentile) is a statistical residual with no per-request enforcement point; a cheap deterministic screen for it is optimistically unsound not only near saturation but well below it. On real 2xV100 the guard holds assured-class miss 0.0 (reps 10, worst upper Wilson CI 0.0053) across every miscalibration of a learned admitter under two shed policies that fail in opposite directions. Against a real, deployed GAIE Flow Control on a serving simulator, a mislabelling router flips the same assured requests from miss 0.0 to 1.0; our guard reserves by the true class, so the label cannot break its floor. We characterize when a cheap static screen can be trusted and when it cannot. As a Frontiers-style submission we evaluate the stance on commodity 2xV100 and a serving simulator, and scope datacenter scale, real-model Flow Control, and a closed worst-case theorem as the agenda.

</details>


### [223] [Descriptive Execution of HPC Applications and Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10081)
*Vanessa Sochat,Daniel Milroy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于大语言模型的自主代理框架在HPC领域的三种任务表现：优化运行AWS上的扩展性实验、转换作业管理器间的任务规格、设计运行生物信息学工作流。代理框架完成了所有任务，但暴露出特定失败模式：扩展实验中使用低效的固定等待策略；作业转换中处理器亲和性标志最容易出错；生物工作流几乎精确复现专家流程（18/19运行一致），并通过多种功能等价路径实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPC领域从人工编写代码向描述性文本转变，基于LLM的自主代理框架开始用于应用编排、作业管理和系统监控。但自主框架的可靠性和任务特定策略的评估尚不充分，需要系统评估其在真实HPC场景中的表现。

Method: 构建LLM+工具函数+资源的自主代理框架，在三个HPC任务上测试：1) AWS低延迟网络上的HPC扩展性研究优化与运行；2) Slurm与Flux作业管理器间的高精度作业规格转换；3) 完整的生物信息学变异检测工作流设计与执行。记录任务完成情况和失败模式。

Result: 框架完成全部三项任务。扩展研究：代理能部署优化应用，但监控效率低，倾向保守固定等待而非事件订阅。作业转换：Slurm与Flux间转换准确率高，处理器亲和性标志是最常见错误。生物工作流：几乎完全复现专家编写的变异检测流程，18/19次运行与参考集一致，通过多种功能等价的实现路径达到相同结果。

Conclusion: 代理框架在HPC任务中展现出可行性和高准确性，但存在任务特定的失败模式。这些信息对未来开发由代理处理调度和转换的多集群HPC系统具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: The means to execute and orchestrate software components has changed from human-written code to descriptive prose. In high performance computing, this transition is represented in application orchestration, workload management, and system monitoring and debugging, to name a few. The underlying means to enable descriptive definition of tasks is the use of the Large Language Model with associated tool functions and resources. A combination of a model with access to such resources, modeled in software, encompasses an autonomous framework. As fully automated and agentic frameworks are developed for science, it is important to assess reliability and strategies scoped to specific tasks. In this work, we assess the extent to which an agentic framework can optimize and run an HPC scaling study with a low latency network in Amazon Web Services, accurately transform HPC job specifications between workload managers, and design and run an entire biosciences workflow. We find that the framework completes all three tasks while surfacing task-specific failure modes. In the scaling study, agents deploy and optimize applications but monitor running jobs inefficiently, preferring conservative fixed waits over event subscriptions. In job translation, they convert specifications between Slurm and Flux with high accuracy, with processor-affinity flags the most common error. In the bioscience workflow, the agent reproduces an expert-written variant-calling pipeline almost exactly -- agreeing with the reference call set in 18 of 19 completed runs -- and reaches this result through many distinct yet functionally equivalent workflow implementations. This information is invaluable moving forward to developing multi-cluster setups with scheduling and transformation handled by agents.

</details>


### [224] [Consensus as Collapse Policy: Communication Evidence, Horizons, and Prefix Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10152)
*Tomislav Grospić*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出将分布式共识协议重新理解为"证据折叠"（collapse under evidence）过程：通信产生二阶证据状态，共识输出是一阶投影；不同协议的区别在于折叠策略（何时、基于何种证据、如何修正）的不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共识协议以终端产出（决定值、复制日志、最终前缀）为中心，这种"输出优先"视角隐藏了使这些产出安全的通信证据。本文的动机是显式化通信产生的证据结构，从而提供统一的比较框架。

Method: 采用指称语义（denotational specification）框架，将分布式执行建模为通信诱导的二阶证据状态，共识输出视为该状态的一阶投影。将不同协议比较为不同的"折叠策略"（collapse policies），并重新解读FLP、set agreement等不可能性结果在这一框架下的含义。

Result: 提供了一个指称规范框架：共识即证据折叠（consensus is collapse under evidence）。FLP并非否定通信不能积累结构，而是约束了在完全异步且一个崩溃故障下确定性保证折叠到终端决策的不可行性；Set agreement暴露了输出载体的宽度。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献不是新的不可能性定理或替代协议特定证明，而是一个统一的指称规范框架，将共识理解为基于证据的折叠过程。

Abstract: Consensus protocols are usually specified by their terminal artifact: a decided value, replicated log, or finalized prefix. This output-first view hides the communication-derived evidence that makes such artifacts safe. This paper makes that carrier explicit: distributed execution is read as an order-2 evidence state induced by communication, while classical consensus outputs are order-1 projections of that state.
  Under this view, consensus protocols can be compared as collapse policies. A protocol specifies which evidence is legitimate, which finite horizon it inspects, when it projects communication evidence into a value or prefix, and how it repairs or defers collapse when the visible evidence is insufficient. The impossibility lineage supports the same distinction. FLP is not a statement that communication cannot accumulate structure; it constrains deterministic guaranteed collapse to a terminal decision under full asynchrony with one crash failure. Set agreement then exposes the width of the output carrier, and topological distributed computing asks when a history/view carrier admits a structure-preserving map to an output carrier. The contribution is not a new impossibility theorem or a replacement for protocol-specific proofs, but a denotational specification framework: consensus is collapse under evidence.

</details>


### [225] [Automated Tensor Scheduling for Hybrid CPU-GPU LLM Inference on Consumer Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10183)
*Yangyijian Liu,Hongyi Ye,Mingyang Li,Wu-jun Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ATSInfer是一个面向消费级设备的混合CPU-GPU推理系统，通过张量级粒度的卸载策略，结合静态放置与负载感知的动态传输，以及异步CPU-GPU协调，显著提升了本地LLM部署的预填充和解码吞吐量（最高提升1.94倍和3.29倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在消费级设备上运行大语言模型时，模型权重常超出GPU内存容量，需借助CPU内存进行卸载推理。但现有卸载系统多采用粗粒度的层级或专家级调度，忽略了同一层内张量间的异构性，且难以适应消费设备上动态变化的硬件负载条件。

Method: ATSInfer提出张量级粒度的卸载策略，结合静态张量放置与负载感知的动态传输，并引入异步CPU-GPU协调机制，以高效调度异构后端间的存储、数据移动和计算资源。

Result: 在代表性消费平台上使用稠密模型和MoE模型进行评估，相比现有系统，预填充吞吐量提升最高达1.94倍，解码吞吐量提升最高达3.29倍，同时提高了GPU利用率和PCIe带宽利用率。

Conclusion: ATSInfer通过细粒度的张量级卸载和动态调度，显著改善了消费级设备上本地LLM部署的用户体验。

Abstract: Running large language models on consumer devices such as laptops and desktops is challenging because model weights often exceed GPU memory capacity, making offloading inference necessary to extend effective model capacity with CPU memory. Existing offloading systems, however, typically rely on coarse layer-level or expert-level scheduling, which overlooks substantial heterogeneity among tensors within the same layer and adapts poorly to changing hardware load conditions on such devices. This paper presents ATSInfer, a hybrid CPU-GPU inference system for consumer devices that performs offloading at tensor granularity. ATSInfer combines static tensor placement with load-aware dynamic transfer, and introduces asynchronous CPU-GPU coordination to efficiently schedule hardware storage, data movement, and computation across heterogeneous backends. We implement ATSInfer and evaluate it on representative consumer platforms using both dense and MoE models. Compared with existing systems, ATSInfer improves prefill throughput by up to 1.94$\times$ and decode throughput by up to 3.29$\times$, while also increasing GPU utilization and making more effective use of PCIe bandwidth. These results show that ATSInfer can substantially improve the user experience of local LLM deployment on personal consumer devices.

</details>


### [226] [Byzantine Accountability Without Consensus: Strong Eventual Consistency for Non-Associative, Stochastic, Robust Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10305)
*Ryan Gillespie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ACFA（Accountable Consensus-Free Aggregation），一种无需共识的拜占庭鲁棒聚合协议。通过CRDT组合（内容寻址OR-Set与仅增长的证据集）实现确定性纯函数聚合，继承强最终一致性。原型在10节点3拜占庭环境下通过16/16验证检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拜占庭鲁棒聚合规则（如multi-Krum）依赖中央协调器，难以去中心化。这些规则具有全局耦合、非结合、不连续的特性，微小扰动可翻转所选子集导致输出大幅变化。去中心化复制需要无共识的贡献顺序约定，需要收敛的集合与排除谓词。

Method: ACFA复制内容寻址OR-Set（已签名贡献）和仅增长的自认证等证证明集。聚合是收敛产品状态的确定性纯函数：采用固定点整数算术、哈希规范顺序、内容哈希断链。证明任意CRDT收敛产品的纯函数（非单调、非结合或随机）继承强最终一致性及其逆命题。核心是数据格与证据格组合应用于鲁棒选择器。

Result: 原型（10节点，3拜占庭）通过16/16验证检查：对抗性八卦下的字节相同根、延迟等证证明后的确定性重新收敛、分区恢复、以及三个破坏字节一致性的消融实验。

Conclusion: 方法保证一致性而非准确性；鲁棒性有条件地继承，需满足2f+3个被接纳贡献（至多f个拜占庭）及指定的量化边际条件。聚合函数是CRDT组合的纯函数，无需共识即可实现拜占庭鲁棒性。

Abstract: Byzantine-robust aggregation rules such as multi-Krum assume a central coordinator, and decentralising them is obstructed by the rules themselves: they are globally coupled, non-associative, and discontinuous, so an ulpscale perturbation can flip the selected subset, moving the output by a non-vanishing amount. None of this prevents coordinator-free replication, because a robust rule needs no agreed order of contributions, only an agreed set and an agreed exclusion predicate, both of which converge without consensus. ACFA (Accountable Consensus-Free Aggregation) replicates a content-addressed OR-Set of signed contributions and a grow-only set of self-authenticating equivocation proofs, offline-verifiable by anyone. Aggregation is a deterministic pure function of the converged product state: fixed-point integer arithmetic over a hash-canonical order, ties broken by content hash. We prove that any pure function of a converged product of CRDTs (non-monotone, non-associative, or stochastic) inherits Strong Eventual Consistency, together with its converse; the contribution is the composition of a data lattice with an evidence lattice applied to a robust selector, not the elementary lifting step. A prototype (10 nodes, 3 Byzantine) passes 16/16 falsification checks: byte-identical roots under adversarial gossip, deterministic re-convergence after late equivocation proofs, partition recovery, and three byte-identity-breaking ablations. The guarantee is consistency, not accuracy; robustness is imported, conditional on 2f + 3 admitted contributions (at most f Byzantine) and a stated quantisation-margin condition.

</details>


### [227] [Stateful Worlds, Stateless Elasticity: Exact-State Serving for Interactive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10389)
*Jin Li,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldMove系统，将交互式世界模型的GPU驻留状态（多GB注意力缓存）在18.8毫秒内以比特精确方式在同节点迁移，比保存/加载快101倍；在100Gb光纤上达到92.1-94.8 Gb/s校验和验证吞吐。该系统通过可接纳性条件与拥塞感知控制器实现集群调度，在48次跨提供商迁移中全部保持比特精确。核心洞察是：交互式世界模型的巨大GPU状态无法在交互时间内重计算或近似，必须进行实时精确迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型的持续运行状态（多GB注意力缓存）驻留GPU且每一生成步骤几乎全部重写，无法在交互时间内重计算或近似，导致活会话固定于设备成为调度问题。现有保存/加载方式太慢，无法满足交互时间要求。

Method: WorldMove：在保证目标与源比特精确的前提下重定位缓存（同节点18.8ms）；校验和验证的92.1-94.8 Gb/s吞吐（100Gb光纤）；活动会话在块边界收敛后目标继续比特精确运行；可接纳性条件决定每次迁移（需在读出视界内完成，带宽覆盖状态加脏率）；拥塞感知接纳控制器（1.4倍负载零遗漏，过载以拒绝释放）；无损GPU编解码器扩展可接纳性。

Result: 同节点缓存迁移18.8ms（比保存/加载快101倍）；100Gb光纤上校验和验证吞吐92.1-94.8 Gb/s；48次跨提供商迁移全部比特精确；拥塞感知控制器在1.4倍负载下零遗漏，过载以拒绝释放。

Conclusion: 精确状态弹性是传输与验证的联合调度问题。两个结构性约束：比特精确性仅在单一GPU架构的受控配置中存活；验证不能隐藏于光纤中（接收路径校验和在扇入下以协议时间尺度阻塞传输，未调度的incast静默压垮接收器）。无损GPU编解码器扩展了原始传输无法使用的可接纳性。服务循环与迁移器已分别端到端验证，但二者在同一光纤上的组合尚未构建。

Abstract: A persistent interactive world model keeps its running state resident on the GPU that serves it: a multi-gigabyte attention cache, almost all of it rewritten at every generation step. That state cannot be recomputed in interactive time or approximated without changing the world, so a live session pins its device. The pin is a scheduling problem. WorldMove moves a live session under one guarantee: the destination is bit-identical to the source, or nothing is installed. It relocates the cache in 18.8 ms same-node, 101x faster than save/load. It holds a checksum-verified 92.1-94.8 Gb/s on a 100 Gb fabric. At that rate the cache fits inside one interactive block. Migrating an actively generating session, it converges at a block boundary and the destination continues the world bit for bit. An admissibility condition decides each move. The move must complete inside the readout horizon, over bandwidth that covers the state plus its dirty rate. Lifted to a fleet schedulability test, it governed a consolidation loop that executed 48 of 48 migrations bit-identical across two providers. Two constraints are structural. Bit-exactness survives only inside a controlled configuration of one GPU architecture, so moving the state is the only way to preserve it exactly in interactive time. Verification cannot hide inside the wire on this fabric. Receive-path checksums stall the transport at protocol timescales under fan-in, and unscheduled incast silently collapses a receiver while every delivered byte stays correct. An incast-aware admission controller holds zero misses to 1.4x offered load and sheds overload as rejects. A lossless GPU codec widens the admission gate to fabrics raw motion cannot use. We exercise the serving loop and the mover separately, each end to end. Their composition on one fabric is unbuilt. Exact-state elasticity is a joint scheduling problem over transport and verification.

</details>


### [228] [CloudMicroHaskell: Direct-Style Distributed Haskell via Runtime Graph Serialisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10443)
*Robert Krook,Lennart Augustsson*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CloudMicroHaskell，一种基于MicroHaskell的分布式编程库，通过运行时组合子图序列化实现移动代码的直接风格，无需手动闭包转换，但以静态类型检查转为动态检查为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cloud Haskell在源级API中处理移动代码时存在困难边界：远程进程必须表达为静态闭包，消息必须满足序列化约束，参与节点需共享相关代码。这些限制增加了编程负担。

Method: 基于MicroHaskell运行时，将代码和数据均表示为组合子图，进程或消息跨越节点边界时直接序列化可达图，从而支持直接风格的远程spawn和消息传递。

Result: 实现了节点运行时（远程spawn、消息投递、监视器、退出传播、通用服务器和监管者库），并通过进程/消息基准测试、分布式工作池、文件同步案例及异构微控制器部署进行了评估。

Conclusion: 运行时图序列化显著简化了编程模型，但代价是Cloud Haskell中源级类型保证变为动态检查，且程序员需注意惰性和运行时拥有资源在节点间移动时的处理。

Abstract: Cloud Haskell brings Erlang-style distributed programming to Haskell, but its treatment of mobile code exposes a difficult boundary in the source-level API. Remote processes must be expressed as static closures, messages must satisfy serialisation constraints, and participating nodes are assumed to share the relevant code.
  This paper explores a different design point. We present CloudMicroHaskell, a Cloud Haskell-style library built on MicroHaskell, whose runtime represents both code and data as a combinator graph. When a process or message crosses a node boundary, CloudMicroHaskell serialises the reachable graph directly. As a result, remote spawning can be written in direct style: process bodies may capture variables from their surrounding scope, and messages may contain ordinary values, including functions, without programmer-written closure conversion.
  We describe the implementation of the CloudMicroHaskell node runtime, including remote spawn, message delivery, monitors, exit propagation, and library implementations of generic servers and supervisors. We evaluate the system with process/message benchmarks, a distributed work-pool benchmark, a file-synchronisation case study, and a heterogeneous deployment on microcontrollers. The results show that runtime graph serialisation makes the \ch{} programming model substantially more direct, while also making the tradeoff explicit: some guarantees enforced by \ch{}'s source-level types become dynamic checks, and programmers must be aware of laziness and runtime-owned resources when moving graphs between nodes.

</details>


### [229] [Ichnos+: Estimating the Carbon Footprint of Scientific Workflows Using Fitted Power Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10586)
*Kathleen West,Youssef Moawad,Philipp Thamm,Vasilis Bountris,Giulio Attenni,Magnus Reid,Yehia Elkhatib,Lauritz Thamsen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Ichnos+系统，用于量化Nextflow科学工作流的碳足迹。该系统基于工作流轨迹、节点特定功耗模型和碳强度数据，实现执行后环境足迹估算。在三个计算集群上的评估显示，其能耗估算误差仅为10.8%，显著优于nf-core插件。此外，Ichnos+还能估算隐含排放、水耗和土地使用，并可扩展至Apache Airflow等其他工作流系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具在共享/虚拟化环境中不可用，或仅基于少量通用数据点构建功耗模型，无法准确量化科学工作流的碳足迹。鉴于ICT部门已存在且持续增长的碳排放，迫切需要一种适用于实际环境的碳足迹量化工具。

Method: Ichnos+采用三阶段方法：(1) 基于已有工作流执行轨迹进行后验估算；(2) 构建节点特定的功耗模型； (3) 结合与执行时间对齐的碳强度数据。对比评估使用Intel RAPL硬件级能耗测量和nf-core co2footprint插件（Green Algorithms方法）。

Result: 在三个计算集群上，Ichnos+的能耗估算误差为10.8%，显著优于nf-core插件。该系统还能估算隐含碳排放、水耗和土地使用，并成功扩展至Apache Airflow，保持高精度。

Conclusion: Ichnos+是一种准确、可扩展的科学工作流环境足迹量化系统，不仅覆盖运营碳，还涵盖隐含排放及水资源和土地使用，且可适配不同工作流系统。

Abstract: As data-intensive scientific workflows scale to facilitate the automation of analysis of increasing amounts of data, their resource-intensive and long-running execution incurs significant energy consumption and carbon emissions. Given the already significant and rising emissions from the ICT sector, it is crucial to quantify and understand the carbon footprint of scientific workflows. However, existing tooling is commonly not usable in shared, virtualized environments or resorts to power models that are based on only one or two generic data points.
  To address this gap, this paper presents Ichnos+, a novel system to quantify the environmental footprint of Nextflow scientific workflows. Ichnos+ enables post-hoc footprint estimation based on existing workflow traces, node-specific power models for the computational resources utilized, and carbon intensity data aligned with the execution time. We evaluate Ichnos+ against hardware-level energy measurements obtained using Intel RAPL, and the nf-core co2footprint plugin, which implements the Green Algorithms methodology. We find that Ichnos+ is capable of estimating workflow energy consumption with an estimation error of 10.8% across three compute clusters, significantly outperforming the nf-core plugin. We further show that Ichnos+ extends beyond operational carbon to estimate embodied emissions as well as water and land use. Finally, we demonstrate how Ichnos+ can be extended for another workflow system, Apache Airflow, maintaining a similarly high degree of estimation accuracy.

</details>


### [230] [[AAFLOW+] Stateful Operator Abstraction with Zero-Copy Distributed KV Cache Orchestration for Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10987)
*Arup Kumar Sarker,Alexander James Halpern,Mills Staylor,Aymen Alsaadi,Gregor von Laszewski,Yue Cheng,Shantenu Jha,Geoffrey Fox*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AAFLOW+ 将 KV 缓存作为一等分布式系统对象，通过状态化工作流算子和零拷贝传输感知运行时，在多智能体 LLM 系统中实现长上下文复用，避免重复预填充，显著降低时延和计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体 LLM 系统仍然以文本为中心，智能体之间通过文本传递共享上下文，导致重复预填充（prefill）开销巨大；而 KV 缓存管理通常局限于单机推理场景，未能在分布式多智能体系统中充分利用。

Method: 提出 AAFLOW+，将 KV 缓存提升为一等分布式系统对象；构建通信感知的计算图，同时优化数据、提示词和可复用模型状态；提供 KV 物化、传输、分支、组合和逐出等操作算子；设计零拷贝、传输感知的运行时环境，实现长上下文复用而无需重计算。

Result: TTFT 降低最多 50.2 倍；16 智能体规模下多智能体计算成本降低最多 7.63 倍；KV 内存减少 1.72-6.10 倍；吞吐量提升超过 7.74 倍（基于经验硬件微基准参数化的分析成本模型）。

Conclusion: 在中高带宽网络上，KV 传输优于重计算；KV 状态共享通过替代文本传递，显著提升多智能体 LLM 系统的效率。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly integrate retrieval, planning, and reasoning, but remain fundamentally text-centric, requiring agents to repeatedly recompute shared context through expensive prefill. Although single-request inference is known to be accelerated by KV-cache management, it is usually restricted to local serving scopes. We introduce AAFLOW+, a stateful extension of agentic workflow operators that makes KV cache a first-class distributed systems object. AAFLOW+ builds processes into communication-aware graphs that concurrently optimize data, prompts, and reusable model state. It also provides operators for KV materialization, transfer, fork, composition, and eviction. Its runtime enables zero-copy, transfer-aware execution, allowing agents to reuse long context without recomputation. AAFLOW+ reduces TTFT by up to 50.2x, achieves up to 7.63x reduced multi-agent compute cost at 16-agent scale, reduces KV memory by 1.72-6.10x, and increases throughput by more than 7.74x, based on an analytical cost model parameterized by empirical hardware microbenchmarks. The results demonstrate that KV transmission outperforms recomputation on networks with moderate to high bandwidth, making sure KV-state sharing greatly increases efficiency in multi-agent LLM systems by replacing text passing.

</details>


### [231] [Domain Extension of Lock-Freedom and Wait-Freedom for Group Computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11014)
*Raaghav Ravishankar,Sandeep Kulkarni,Sathya Peri,Gokarna Sharma,Manaswini Piduguralla,Yashi Rastogi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文将有锁自由和无锁自由的定义从单线程任务扩展到多线程协作的群组计算（gl-freedom和gw-freedom），并在保持原始定义一致性的前提下，为分布式计算、NUMA系统等新领域提供了更精确的并发程序分类方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的lock-free和wait-free定义仅适用于单线程完成任务的计算场景，无法准确描述需要多线程协作才能完成的群组计算。作者希望扩展这些定义，以便更精确地刻画分布式计算、NUMA系统、私有数据线程系统等新领域的并发程序特性，而不是简单地将它们归为死锁/活锁自由类别。

Method: 通过domain extension方法论，将lock-free和wait-free的定义从单线程域扩展到多线程协作的群组计算域，提出gl-freedom和gw-freedom。通过约束条件（逻辑一致性、无矛盾歧义、保留本质属性）保证扩展的有效性，并通过单线程情况下的一致性验证来证明扩展的合理性。

Result: 成功定义了gl-freedom和gw-freedom，并验证了当群组仅包含单个线程时，扩展定义与原始lock-free/wait-free定义一致。通过多个示例展示了新定义在分布式计算、NUMA系统、私有数据线程系统等场景中的应用价值。

Conclusion: domain extension方法能够将并发原语的适用域扩展到多线程协作场景，为原本只能被粗粒度归类为死锁/活锁自由的程序提供更精细的特性刻画，具有理论一致性和实践应用价值。

Abstract: A domain extension of a definition refers to broadening the scope of a definition so that it applies to a larger set of cases than originally specified. The notion of lock-free and wait-free computation is designed for the domain of tasks that are completed by a single thread (in competition with other threads). The goal of this paper is to extend the definition of lock-freedom and wait-freedom to group-computations (denoted by gl-freedom and gw-freedom) that require that the task at hand must be completed by a collaboration between multiple threads. When extending a definition, certain constraints must be respected: the new domain must remain logically consistent with the original meaning, the extension should not introduce contradictions or ambiguities, and it must preserve the essential properties that make the definition valid and useful. We demonstrate this by showing that our extended definition is consistent with the original definition when the group consists of a single thread. We note that extension allows us to characterize programs in a new domain (distributed computing, NUMA computation systems, systems with private data for different threads, etc.) instead of relegating them to be in the same category (deadlock/livelock-free) without regard to the actual properties of that program. We also illustrate this definition with various examples.

</details>


### [232] [Xema: Efficient Diffusion Serving through Fine-Grained Memory Management and Auto-Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11136)
*Xueze Kang,Guangyu Xiang,Suyi Li,Yuxin Wang,Shaohuai Shi,Lin Zhang,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Xema是一个内存高效的扩散模型推理服务系统，通过利用张量可预测的生命周期进行迹引导的内存优化，在满足SLO约束的同时显著降低GPU内存使用，相比现有方案将SLO达标率提升最多3.7倍，并将规划成本从6.3小时降至197秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在部署为生产级视觉生成服务时，高分辨率图像和长视频生成常受GPU内存限制；现有内存节省技术（如权重卸载、分片、VAE切片）因引入显著性能开销而难以实用。

Method: Xema提出迹引导的内存优化方法：1) 为每个请求模板推导离线内存迹，识别短内存压力区间并仅在此时按需应用内存缓解措施；2) 为可预测生命周期的张量构建静态内存布局，减少碎片化导致的预留内存；3) 在GPU内存和SLO约束下联合选择并行度、并发度和内存控制策略，生成离线规划表供在线运行时直接使用。

Result: 在Flux.2、CogVideoX-5B和LTX-2等生产级扩散流水线上评估，Xema相比现有服务配置将SLO达标率提升最多3.7倍，规划成本从网格搜索的6.3小时降低至197秒。

Conclusion: Xema通过利用张量可预测生命周期实现精准、低开销的内存优化，为扩散模型的高效生产部署提供了一种实用的系统级解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models are increasingly deployed as production visual-generation services, where serving high-resolution image and long video generation is often limited by GPU memory. Popular memory-saving techniques such as weight offloading, sharding, and VAE slicing are often not practical because they tend to introduce significant performance overhead. In this paper, we present Xema, a memory-efficient diffusion serving system that exploits predictable tensor lifetimes for trace-guided memory optimization. For each request template, Xema derives an offline memory trace to identify short memory-pressure intervals and applies memory mitigation only within these intervals and only by the amount needed to fit the target GPU budget. Xema further constructs a static memory layout for tensors with predictable lifetimes, reducing fragmentation-induced reserved memory and making offline memory reasoning reliable at runtime. Built on this memory optimization layer, Xema introduces an offline planner that jointly selects parallelism, concurrency, and memory control under GPU memory and SLO constraints. The selected plan is stored in a plan table and directly used by the online serving runtime. We implement Xema on production diffusion pipelines and evaluate it with Flux.2, CogVideoX-5B, and LTX-2. Compared with existing serving configurations, Xema improves SLO attainment by up to 3.7x and reduces planning cost from 6.3 hours to 197 seconds compared with grid search.

</details>


### [233] [GPU-Tile-Sim: A Tile-Centric GPU Simulation Framework for LLM Hardware-Software Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11262)
*Yitong Ding,Jiawei Huang,Renyang Guan,Yangjie Zhou,Zihan Liu,Yu Feng,Shixuan Sun,Mingyi Guo,Jingwen Leng,Jian Weng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出GPU-Tile-Sim (GTSim)，一种面向LLM硬件-软件协同设计的以tile为中心的GPU仿真框架。核心思想是将现代LLM内核性能建模为warp级tile图，通过图驱动仿真实现高精度性能预测，在A100/H100上MAPE为1.22%-8.71%，并扩展至Blackwell架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM工作负载依赖硬件-软件协同设计优化的GPU内核，这些内核通过细粒度依赖调度和计算-内存重叠实现高性能，但现有GPU性能模型难以适配：指令驱动模拟器适应新架构成本高，分析模型过于粗糙无法捕捉内核特征。

Method: 将内核执行表示为warp级tile图，节点表示tile级操作，边编码数据和排序约束；设计自动tile图前端和图驱动仿真后端，通过依赖结构而非单条指令延迟来建模性能。

Result: 在A100和H100上对GEMM、注意力机制和端到端LLM推理进行评测，MAPE为1.22%-8.71%；初步扩展至Blackwell架构并验证了其分析软件和架构设计选择的有效性。

Conclusion: GTSim通过tile图抽象实现了高精度、低成本的GPU性能建模，适用于LLM场景下的硬件-软件协同设计分析与优化。

Abstract: Modern LLM (large language model) workloads increasingly rely on optimized GPU kernels through hardware-software co-design. These kernels achieve high-performance through fine-grained dependency scheduling and computation-memory overlap. As such, they incur new challenges on existing GPU performance models. Instruction-driven simulators are costly to adapt to evolving architectures, while analytical models are too coarse to capture kernels' characteristics. We propose GPU-Tile-Sim, a tile-centric GPU simulation framework for LLM hardware-software co-design. The key insight is that modern LLM kernel performance is governed less by individual instruction latency than by the dependency structure that controls execution order and overlap. Accordingly, GTSim represents kernel execution as a warp-level tile graph whose nodes capture tile-level operations and whose edges encode data and ordering constraints. Using this representation, we design an automatic tile-graph frontend and a graph-driven simulation backend. We evaluate GTSim on representative GEMM, attention, and end-to-end LLM inference workloads. On A100 and H100 across both conventional and highly optimized kernels, GTSim achieves high performance-modeling accuracy (MAPE, Mean Absolute Percentage Error, 1.22%--8.71%). We further extend GTSim to Blackwell with preliminary validation, and demonstrate its effectiveness in analyzing software and architectural design choices.

</details>


### [234] [Stage-Level Executor Allocation in Apache Spark with Cost-Performance Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11415)
*Miriam Rateike,Isaac Waweru Wambugu,Celia Cintas,Michael Kaufmann,Ioana Giurgiu,Skyler Speakman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在无服务器Apache Spark环境中按阶段动态分配执行器数量的方法，通过树集成模型预测运行时间和成本，支持用户在成本与性能之间自定义权衡，在TPC-DS和SQLStorm基准测试上实现了约40-50%的成本节约，仅带来16-29%的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式系统执行器分配方法要么在应用级别进行次优分配，无法适应数千个多样化阶段的个性化需求，要么不支持用户对成本-性能权衡的显式偏好（如在特定时间预算内最小化成本），因此需要一种能够按阶段精细化分配资源并支持用户自定义权衡的方法。

Method: 训练树集成模型（如随机森林或梯度提升树）来估计每个阶段在不同资源配置下的运行时间和成本，然后利用这些估计值为每个阶段单独推荐最优执行器数量，从而在用户定义的成本-性能权衡参数下实现全局优化。

Result: 在TPC-DS基准测试的103个查询上，根据用户权衡参数的不同，该方法实现了约50%的成本节约，仅带来约16%的性能下降；在SQLStorm基准测试的96个查询上，实现了约40.5%的成本节约，性能下降约29%。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效支持用户自定义的成本-性能权衡，在无服务器Spark环境中通过按阶段精细分配资源显著降低计算成本，同时保持可接受的性能水平，为云原生数据处理的资源优化提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Allocating executors (i.e. compute resources) to distributed processing systems must balance resource costs of scaling-out unnecessarily against artificial, performance-limiting bottlenecks. Naive approaches may allocate executors at the application level, which have predictable costs and performance but are almost guaranteed to be sub-optimal for each of the thousands of diverse, individual stages executed by the application. Users may also have explicit preferences, such as completing an application within a specific time budget while minimizing cost, that existing solutions usually fail to support. We propose a novel method for determining the number of executors per stage in a serverless Apache Spark environment, enabling users to specify their desired cost-performance tradeoff. Our approach trains tree-ensemble models to estimate the run times and costs of a stage as a function of allocated resources. These estimates are then used to recommend resources for each stage individually. We evaluate our approach on TPC-DS and SQLStorm benchmarks and compare it against two baselines. Depending on the user-defined trade-off parameter and setup, our approach achieves approx. 50% cost savings across 103 TPC-DS queries with only a approx. 16% slowdown, and approx. 40.5% on 96 SQLStorm queries at a approx. 29% slowdown.

</details>


### [235] [MemExchange: Cloud-Scale Memory Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11579)
*AmirHossein Seyri,Abhisek Pan,Balajee Vamanan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MemExchange 是一个集群级、多租户内存管理系统，通过在线缺失率曲线（MRC）估计的边际效用分配和RDMA远程直接内存访问，动态调整内存缓存租户的大小，将分散的缓存内存聚合为逻辑共享池，实现跨节点内存交换，无需集中协调或强制租户共置，在100台CloudLab服务器上验证了高达2.3倍远程访问开销降低、13%集群内存利用率提升和63%缓存缺失率降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云提供商过度预配置内存以应对峰值负载，导致数据中心集群内存利用率低下；同时内存受限的租户即使在其他节点有空闲内存时仍遭受高缓存缺失率，内存资源无法跨节点灵活调度。

Method: 基于在线MRC估计的边际效用分配方法动态调整内存分配；设计MTC协议实现应用层协调与单边RDMA操作，避免远程CPU参与；构建跨节点的逻辑聚合内存池。

Result: 相比TCP方案远程访问开销降低2.3倍；机架级集群内存利用率提升13%；偏斜负载下内存受限租户的缓存缺失率降低63%。

Conclusion: MemExchange通过边际效用分配和RDMA远程内存交换有效解决了云环境中的内存资源碎片化和利用不均衡问题，在不强制租户共置的前提下实现了高效的内存资源池化。

Abstract: To handle unpredictable workloads, cloud providers typically over-provision memory to meet peak demand, resulting in substantial underutilization across datacenter clusters. At the same time, memory-constrained tenants may suffer elevated cache miss rates, even when idle capacity remains stranded elsewhere in the infrastructure. MemExchange is a cluster-wide, multi-tenant memory management system that dynamically right-sizes in-memory caching tenants according to workload demand. Leveraging marginal-utility-based allocation derived from online Miss Ratio Curve (MRC) estimation, MemExchange redistributes idle memory between tenants across physical nodes using RDMA. This approach transforms the dedicated caching memory scattered across servers into a logically aggregated pool, enabling cross-node memory exchange without centralized coordination or forced tenant co-location. To support efficient remote access, we design the MemExchange Tracker Communication (MTC) protocol, an application-layer mechanism that coordinates memory reallocation and enables one-sided RDMA operations without involving remote CPUs. We implement MemExchange in Memcached and evaluate it through microbenchmarks, medium and rack-scale deployments of up to 100 CloudLab servers. Our results show up to 2.3x lower remote-access overhead compared to TCP-based designs, a 13% increase in cluster-wide memory utilization at rack scale, and up to 63% reduction in miss rate for memory-constrained tenants under skewed workloads.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [Knowledge Graphs Meet Graph Neural Networks: A Comprehensive Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09666)
*Chengcheng Sun,Jiayun Tian,Cheng Zhai,Zhixiao Wang,Yajie Song,Xiaobin Rui,Jian Zhang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对知识图谱中图神经网络方法的双层次分类框架（KG技术流水线视角和GNN模型视角），系统综述了GNN在知识图谱构建、嵌入、推理和应用中的研究现状，分析了各任务的优缺点，并指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏对GNN方法在知识图谱全技术流水线中的系统性综述，现有研究多聚焦于单一任务或模型，未能提供全局性的分类与对比分析。

Method: 提出双层次分类框架：第一层按KG技术流水线（构建、嵌入、推理、应用）分类，第二层按GNN模型类型（GCN、GAT、HGNN等）分类；基于该框架对现有GNN-based KG方法进行详细梳理与对比分析。

Result: 构建了系统性的双层次分类体系，覆盖了知识图谱全生命周期中的GNN方法，总结了不同任务场景下GNN模型的优势与局限性。

Conclusion: GNN在知识图谱各环节均展现出显著优势，但仍面临可解释性、动态性、大规模图处理等挑战，未来需探索更高效的模型架构和跨任务迁移方法。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm in Knowledge Graphs (KGs) due to their intrinsic ability to model graph-structured data. However, there remains a lack of a systematic review about GNN-based methodologies across the entire knowledge graph technologies pipeline. To address this gap, we first propose a novel two-level taxonomy framework for GNN-based knowledge graph technologies: the KG technologies pipeline and GNN-based perspective. Specifically, the knowledge graph technologies pipeline covers knowledge graph construction, knowledge graph embedding, knowledge reasoning and knowledge graph applications. Meanwhile, the GNN-based perspective provides a new categorization of knowledge graph technologies with GNN models, such as GCN, GAT, and HGNN. Then, we analyze the advantages of GNN technology based on the characteristics of different tasks in the knowledge graph lifecycle. Furthermore, we detailed review various GNN-based models for knowledge graph following the proposed taxonomy, and summarize strengths and limitations. Finally, we discuss unresolved challenges and outline promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [237] [Position: Every Ground Truth is a Human Construction, not an Objective Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09668)
*Charlotte Högberg,Ericka Johnson,Kiri L. Wagstaff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了机器学习中的"地面真值"数据集并非客观中立的自然给定，而是由人类与技术共同建构的产物；ML社区需要承认这些参考数据集的情境依赖性和局限性，通过提升"情境可靠性"来改善模型的可信度、透明度与问责性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战ML领域中将ground truth视为客观、中立、自然给定的普遍假设，揭示其背后被忽视的人为建构过程与选择，从而推动更可靠、透明和负责任的ML实践。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）的论证方式，通过理论分析阐述ground truth的情境性与建构性，提出"情境可靠性"（situated reliability）概念作为替代性评估框架。

Result: 揭示了ground truth数据集的人为建构本质及其隐含的偶然性；指出articulating（阐明）数据集与模型的适用边界能提升可靠性；提出关注ground truth建构过程有助于促进透明度、问责性和跨学科合作。

Conclusion: ML社区应转变对ground truth的认知，承认其情境依赖性和建构性，通过明确数据集的适用范围和局限性来实现"情境可靠性"，而非追求普适的、去情境化的客观标准。

Abstract: Ground truth datasets play a fundamental role as reference values in the training and evaluation of machine learning models. This position paper argues that ground truths are not neutral objective measurements that are naturally given, but instead that they are constructed by arrangements of humans and technologies. We argue that the ML community will benefit from articulating and discussing these often invisible or unreported choices and acknowledging that reference data sets are contingent, not universal. Focusing on the situated and context-dependent nature of ground truths can improve reliability by enabling a better informed perspective on where, when, and how the datasets, and the models they have shaped, can best be used. We argue for increasing `situated reliability' which includes articulating the limits and strengths of models and their truth claims. Finally, paying more attention to the construction of ground truths can support transparency, accountability, and interdisciplinary work.

</details>


### [238] [AuditWeave: A Tamper-Evident, Auditor-Navigable Evidence Layer for AI-Assisted and Data-Transformation Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09682)
*Vimal Nakrani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuditWeave是一个轻量级Python库，通过仅追加的哈希链账本记录AI辅助和数据转换工作流步骤，支持端到端追溯和篡改检测，开销为微秒级且在2000次随机试验中成功检测所有注入的篡改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具面向机器学习工程师而非审查者，无法满足审计、金融、医疗等受监管领域中"事后追溯哪些证据支撑了特定结论"及"证明推理记录未被篡改"的合规需求。

Method: 设计了一个轻量级Python库AuditWeave，采用仅追加的哈希链账本结构，定义了一套系统无关的小型事件词汇表，覆盖RAG管道和表格/数据湖转换，实现端到端追溯；通过链式验证检测任何修改、重排、插入或删除事件。

Result: 每个事件的开销为数十微秒；在2000次随机试验中，针对四种篡改类别的链式验证全部成功检测到注入的篡改。

Conclusion: AuditWeave能够以极低开销提供可验证的完整性保证，适用于需要事后审计和合规性证明的AI辅助决策场景。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly used to assist consequential decisions in regulated domains such as auditing, finance, and healthcare. This creates a recurring obligation: an organization must be able to reconstruct, after the fact, which evidence informed a given conclusion, and to show that the record of that reasoning was not altered. Existing tools address related but distinct problems - model observability, drift monitoring, governance reporting - and are built for the machine-learning engineer operating a system, not the reviewer who must trace one specific conclusion back to its supporting evidence. We present AuditWeave, a lightweight Python library, with no runtime dependencies, that records the steps of AI-assisted and data-transformation workflows into a single append-only, hash-chained ledger. A small, system-agnostic event vocabulary spans both retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines and tabular/lakehouse transformations, so a conclusion that draws on both can be traced end-to-end through one record. Within a sealed ledger, any modification, reordering, insertion, or deletion of events is detectable through chain verification. We describe the design and evaluate recording overhead, scalability, and tamper-detection correctness on the reference implementation. The integrity guarantees cost tens of microseconds per event, and, as the hash-chain construction implies, verification flagged every injected mutation across four mutation classes over 2,000 randomized trials.

</details>


### [239] [Ablation, Statistical Inference, and Validation for KV-Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09683)
*Paolo D'Alberto,Ashish Siarasao,Elliott Delaye,Rajeev Patwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了Turbo-Quant和SpectralQuant两种KV-cache压缩方法，通过统计验证方法分离系统编解码差异与实现方差，评估了包含WHT旋转+Beta Lloyd-Max和QJL在内的非支配方案。关键发现：基于特征基的方法在重尾数据上因协方差不稳定而失败，但在结构化数据中表现优异；有效语义维度(d_eff)会随校准预算调整，而非反映真实数据秩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV-cache压缩方法的比较缺乏系统性的统计验证，难以区分编解码算法本身的差异与实现细节带来的方差，需要建立更严谨的评估框架。

Method: 采用统计验证方法论，系统比较Turbo-Quant和SpectralQuant两种压缩方案，并评估WHT旋转+Beta Lloyd-Max和QJL等非支配方案，通过分离系统差异与实现方差进行公平对比。

Result: 1) 基于特征基的方法在重尾数据上因协方差估计不稳定而失败；2) 在结构化数据场景下特征基方法表现优异；3) 有效语义维度(d_eff)随校准预算变化，而非反映真实数据秩。

Conclusion: 选择KV-cache压缩方法需根据数据特性（重尾vs结构化）权衡，且有效语义维度是受校准预算影响的动态指标，不能简单视为数据的固有属性。

Abstract: This study systematically compares Turbo-Quant and SpectralQuant KV-cache compression, evaluating non-dominated schemes, including WHT rotation with Beta Lloyd-Max and QJL, through a statistical validation methodology that separates systematic codec differences from implementation variance. Key findings reveal that while eigenbasis-based methods fail on heavy-tailed data due to covariance instability, they excel in structured regimes, with the effective semantic dimension ($d_{eff}$) adapting to calibration budgets rather than true data rank. (this is an abstract of the abstract thank you )

</details>


### [240] [SciML in the Wild: A Diagnostic Study of When Structural Priors Help and When They Hurt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09684)
*Vrishank Sai Anand,Prathamesh Dinesh Joshi,Raj Abhijit Dandekar,Rajat Dandekar,Sreedath Panat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了科学机器学习（SciML）方法在宏观经济预测这一低频、稀疏数据领域的表现，发现结构先验知识若与数据生成过程不匹配，会起到"错误正则化"的负面作用，导致更少约束的模型（ARIMA、NODE）反而优于强约束模型（PINN、UDE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当SciML方法依赖的结构性先验知识（如物理规律、已知动力学）不成立时，模型性能会受到怎样的影响。以宏观经济预测作为压力测试领域，因为该领域的"真实"动力学机制往往是未知且不稳定的。

Method: 在23个国家的稀疏年度数据上，使用多个时间划分和5个随机种子，系统评估了五个模型家族：ARIMA、LSTM、神经ODE（NODE）、物理信息神经网络（PINN）和通用微分方程（UDE）。

Result: 没有任何模型在所有场景下实现稳定的强预测性能；但存在明确的相对层级：较少约束的模型（ARIMA、NODE）一致优于受启发式先验约束更强的模型（PINN、UDE）。

Conclusion: 结构先验如果与数据生成过程不匹配，会变成"错误正则化器"（misregularizer）。SciML实践者在使用结构先验前应检验其是否真正有益，而非默认"更多结构=更好"。识别的失败模式包括先验错配、制度变迁、结构性断点和优化不稳定。

Abstract: Scientific Machine Learning (SciML) methods such as Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (NODEs), Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), and Universal Differential Equations (UDEs) are most effective when structural priors reflect reliable governing dynamics. We ask what happens when this assumption is violated. Using macroeconomic forecasting as a stress-test domain, we evaluate five model families, ARIMA, LSTM, NODE, PINN, and UDE, across 23 countries using sparse annual data, multiple temporal splits, and five random seeds. Our results show that none of the evaluated models achieve consistently strong forecasting performance, highlighting the difficulty of low-frequency macroeconomic prediction. However, a clear relative hierarchy emerges: less-constrained models, particularly ARIMA and NODE, consistently outperform more-constrained heuristic-prior models such as PINN and UDE. Rather than treating this as a rejection of SciML, we interpret it as a diagnostic result: structural priors can act as misregularizers when they do not match the data-generating process. We identify failure modes including prior misalignment, regime shifts, structural breaks, and optimization instability, and argue that SciML practitioners should test whether structure helps before assuming that more structure is beneficial.

</details>


### [241] [MawForge: Memory-Bounded Expert Materialization for Local Mixture-of-Experts Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09686)
*Craig Opie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MawForge提出了一种在统一内存设备上本地运行MoE模型的新方案：将完整模型存储在磁盘中，仅将常用张量常驻内存，并通过按需将路由专家张量加载到有限执行缓存中，从而在受限硬件上实现MoE推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型虽能分离总参数量与每次激活的计算量，但本地推理系统仍需要将完整模型、KV缓存、运行时缓冲区及操作系统开销全部容纳于快速内存中，在统一内存的受限设备上难以实用。

Method: 将完整模型存储在磁盘上，保持常用张量常驻内存，将路由专家张量按需物化到有界执行缓存中，并作为测量实验平台探索专家重用、驻留内存、KV缓存大小、量化、路由局部性和macOS内存压力等因素的平衡。

Result: MawForge作为有界执行机制和本地MoE推理的测量基座是有效的，但作为缓存最大化策略效果不佳；性能取决于多因素的平衡。

Conclusion: MawForge验证了在统一内存受限设备上通过磁盘驻留+按需加载实现MoE本地推理的可行性，但缓存策略本身并非最优，需要针对多因素进行调优。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models separate total parameter count from per-token active computation, but local inference systems often still require the full model, key-value cache, runtime buffers, and operatingsystem headroom to fit in fast memory. MawForge tests a different systems hypothesis: local MoE serving can be made practical on constrained unified-memory machines by storing the full model on disk, keeping common tensors resident, and materializing routed expert tensors into a bounded execution cache on demand. The central finding is that MawForge is effective as a bounded execution mechanism and measurement substrate for local MoE inference, but not as a cache-maximization policy. Performance depends on balancing expert reuse against resident footprint, KV-cache size, quantization, route locality, and macOS memory pressure.

</details>


### [242] [Prioritizing Search Space Regions in the Low Autocorrelation Binary Sequences Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09688)
*Blaž Pšeničnik,Borko Bošković,Jan Popić,Janez Brest*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Thompson采样与并行自回避行走的混合搜索框架，通过多臂老虎机模型自适应分配计算资源，在LABS问题上取得了35个序列长度（450≤L≤527及L=573）的新的最优结果，并首次在L=451时获得了超过8.0的优值因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低自相关二元序列问题（LABS）是通信、信号处理和卫星导航等领域中的重要组合优化难题，现有方法在长序列上难以获得高优值因子，亟需更高效的搜索策略。

Method: 提出混合搜索框架：1) 将搜索空间按分区建模为多臂老虎机，用Thompson采样动态分配计算资源；2) 采用并行自回避行走进行局部搜索；3) GPU并行执行、共享后验更新、高效邻域评估、Bloom过滤器防循环；4) 两阶段优化策略：先搜索约束分区的斜对称空间，再在无约束空间中优化最佳候选。

Result: 在450≤L≤527范围内35个序列长度及L=573上改进了此前最优结果；L=451时获得优值因子超过8.0的新最长序列；Thompson采样能有效优先分配资源到表现更好的分区。

Conclusion: 该框架通过在线数据驱动的资源分配策略，为LABS优化提供了一种可扩展且有效的方案，验证了自适应资源分配在高性能优值因子最大化中的价值。

Abstract: Low autocorrelation binary sequences problem (LABS) is a hard combinatorial optimization challenge with important applications in communications, signal processing, and satellite navigation. This paper proposes a hybrid search framework that combines Thompson sampling with parallel self-avoiding walks to adaptively allocate computational effort across restriction classes of the LABS search space. By modeling partitions as arms in a multi-armed bandit setting, the proposed method dynamically shifts search resources toward partitions that empirically produce higher merit factors while maintaining exploration of less-sampled regions. The approach is further accelerated through GPU-parallel execution, shared posterior updates, efficient neighborhood evaluation, and a Bloom filter for cycle prevention. In addition, we use a two-stage optimization strategy that first searches constrained partitioned skew-symmetric spaces and then refines the best candidates in the unrestricted space. Experiments on long binary sequences show that the proposed method improves the previously best-known results for 35 sequence lengths in the range $450 \le L \le 527$ and for $L=573$. In particular, we report a new longest sequence with merit factor exceeding $8.0$, obtained for $L=451$. The results also show that Thompson sampling effectively prioritizes partitions with better observed performance, confirming the value of online, data-driven resource allocation in LABS optimization. Overall, the proposed framework provides a scalable and effective strategy for high-performance merit factor maximization.

</details>


### [243] [What Context Does a Coding Agent Actually Need to Act?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09691)
*Brian Sam-Bodden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究编码代理在编辑代码时真正需要什么上下文信息。通过分离"定位"与"编辑"两个阶段，作者发现：信号主要来自被编辑代码本身（而非其自然语言摘要），周围上下文影响很小（UML骨架与删除剩余上下文效果相当），压缩上下文（19K tokens）即可达到全文件（94K tokens）的效果。还发现温度0的API推理会产生约9%的结果噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理能处理整个代码仓库，但大部分阅读是浪费的。作者想探究一个关键问题：代理在编辑代码时，真正需要什么信息？并非关注它能利用多少上下文，而是它实际需要多少。

Method: 将"定位"（寻找修改点）与"执行"（实际编辑）分离，通过Oracle固定定位环节，仅变化代码表示方式。在SWE-bench Verified上，用真实issue解决率作为评分标准。测试了自然语言摘要vs源代码、UML骨架vs删除剩余上下文、压缩上下文vs完整文件等对比条件。

Result: 1）源代码本身包含关键信号，自然语言摘要几乎无法回答源能回答的行为问题（4/45 vs 27/45），且该差距来自表示形式而非摘要模型能力；2）UML骨架与直接删除周围上下文在解决问题上没有显著差异（p=0.75）；3）压缩上下文（19K tokens）即可达到全文件（94K tokens）的效果；4）温度0的API推理在字节相同的运行间有约9%的结果翻转。

Conclusion: 编码代理在编辑代码时真正需要的上下文远少于预期——关键在于被编辑的代码本身，而非其自然语言描述或周围上下文。压缩上下文可以大幅减少token消耗。同时，温度0推理的噪声下限对基于SWE-bench的小效应研究提出了警示。

Abstract: A modern coding agent can hold an entire repository in its context window. Most of its reading is wasted -- and the interesting question is not how much context an agent can use, but what it actually \emph{needs}. We study that question at the moment it matters most: when the agent must \emph{edit} code. Separating \emph{finding} the work site from \emph{acting} on it, we hold localization fixed with an oracle, vary only how the code is represented, and score context against real issue resolution on SWE-bench Verified. The answer is starkly minimal. The signal lives in the code being edited itself: natural-language summaries of it answer almost none of the behavioral questions that the source answers ($4/45$ vs.\ $27/45$, held-out repositories, independent judge), and the gap belongs to the representation, not the summarizer -- a frontier model's summaries score exactly as poorly as a 3B model's. The surrounding context hardly matters either: across every multi-file instance in Verified, under a protocol frozen before any data, rendering a file's remainder as UML skeletons and signatures resolves no more issues than deleting that remainder outright ($N{=}70$, exact McNemar $p{=}0.75$). That was our registered hypothesis, and it failed. Compressed context, meanwhile, matches whole files at a third of the tokens: a resolved issue costs $19$K context tokens, not $94$K. The instrument also yielded a finding the field should keep: temperature-0 API inference flips ${\sim}9\%$ of per-instance outcomes between byte-identical runs. That is a noise floor under every small effect reported on this benchmark, including ours. We release the instrument -- gold-validated environments, per-instance proof that every reference edit is expressible from every arm's context, deterministic patch construction, and pre-registered hypotheses whose nulls we publish.

</details>


### [244] [Reference-Based Distillation Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09692)
*Rajat Rawat,Sizhe Chen,Akshay Anand,Michael Duan,Bob Rotsted,Sewon Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于参考的成员推理方法，用于检测模型是否从其他模型蒸馏而来。通过比较学生模型对不同候选教师输出的对齐程度，该方法能以近完美精度识别真实教师，并成功应用于QwQ、DeepSeek-R1和GPT-OSS等当代模型的蒸馏关系分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型蒸馏广泛使用但引发不公平优势和违反政策的问题，因此需要检测模型是否来自蒸馏。然而，从孤立模型中识别教师极具挑战性。

Method: 提出基于参考的成员推理检测方法：通过比较学生模型对不同候选教师输出的对齐强度（相对于参考检查点），识别最可能的教师模型。针对未知蒸馏流程（如隐藏prompt），从模型输出推断代理prompt模板。还识别了o1/o3模型特有的字形级信号。

Result: 在单教师蒸馏场景下，该方法以近完美精度恢复真实教师，即使底层蒸馏流程大部分未知。成功应用于QwQ、DeepSeek-R1和GPT-OSS等当代模型，发现了新的潜在蒸馏关系证据。

Conclusion: 提出的参考设置使蒸馏检测变得可行，并提供了统计检验和开放世界扩展。实验结果证明了方法的有效性，并为分析当代大模型之间的蒸馏关系提供了新工具。

Abstract: Model distillation -- training on outputs from stronger third-party models -- is widely used to boost performance, but raises concerns about unfair advantages and policy violations. This motivates a fundamental question: can we detect whether a model was distilled from another? We show that, while identifying a teacher model from a student in isolation is highly challenging, it becomes tractable in a reference-based setting: given a model and an earlier-generation checkpoint from the same lineage, we can identify the teacher model used to train the later checkpoint. We introduce a distillation detection method based on reference-based membership inference. By comparing how strongly a student model preferentially aligns with outputs from different candidate teachers relative to a reference checkpoint, our method identifies the most likely teacher and detects evidence of distillation. To handle unknown distillation pipelines such as hidden prompts, we infer proxy prompt templates directly from model outputs. We additionally identify a distinctive glyph-level signal specific to o1/o3 models. Evaluating distillation detection is challenging because modern model lineages are already heavily entangled. To address this, we develop a hybrid evaluation spanning both controlled distillation experiments and real-world models. Across both settings, our approach recovers the true teacher with near-perfect accuracy in single-teacher distillation scenarios, even when the underlying distillation pipeline is largely unknown. We further introduce statistical tests for both teacher attribution and distillation detection, and extend our framework to open-world settings where no teacher is guaranteed to be present among the candidates. Applying our method to contemporary models yields new evidence regarding potential distillation relationships involving QwQ, DeepSeek-R1, and GPT-OSS.

</details>


### [245] [Depth-Entropy Guided Sampling for Training-Free LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09693)
*Zibin Meng,Peng Xie,Kani Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DEGS，一种无需训练、仅靠测试时利用Transformer层间熵坍塌作为内在质量信号的采样方法，在推理任务上取得接近甚至超越RL后训练模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL虽能提升LLM推理能力，但需要昂贵训练、精心标注数据和奖励信号；现有测试时采样方法仅依赖输出层似然，忽略了transformer内部前向传播动力学中的有用信息。

Method: 利用logit-lens解码各层熵值，定义序列级别的坍塌深度D(x)，将序列似然与深度熵结构结合为联合目标π(x) ∝ p(x)^α exp(βD(x))，并实例化为MCMC功率采样框架DEGS-MCMC。

Result: 在三个开源模型和四个推理基准上，DEGS实现了无需训练的最先进准确率；在数学分项上略微落后于GRPO，但在GPQA上对所有三个模型均超越GRPO，且仅增加个位数百分比的墙钟开销。

Conclusion: 层间熵坍塌可作为有效的测试时内在质量信号，在无需训练、奖励模型或标注数据的情况下，补偿甚至超越RL后训练带来的部分推理增益，尤其在域外和困难子集上优势明显。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become the dominant paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but it requires expensive training, curated data, and reward signals. Recent work shows that sampling from sharpened base-model distributions at test time recovers much of the RL gain, yet existing methods rely solely on output-layer likelihoods and ignore the transformer's internal forward-pass dynamics. We introduce Depth-Entropy Guided Sampling (DEGS), a training-free, test-time method that exploits layer-wise entropy collapse as an intrinsic quality signal. We observe that stronger reasoners -- including RL-posttrained variants -- exhibit a distinctive "late collapse": logit-lens decoded entropy stays elevated until deeper layers before converging. We define a per-sequence collapse depth $D(\mathbf{x})$ and a joint objective $π(\mathbf{x}) \propto p(\mathbf{x})^α\exp(βD(\mathbf{x}))$ that combines sequence likelihood with this depth-entropy structure, instantiated inside an MCMC power-sampling framework (DEGS-MCMC). Across three open-weight models and four reasoning benchmarks, this near-chance per-candidate signal compounds over the sampling trajectory into state-of-the-art training-free accuracy, with gains largest out of domain and on the harder splits -- exactly where likelihood alone falls short -- at single-digit-percent wall-clock overhead. DEGS narrowly trails an in-house GRPO reference on the math splits GRPO was trained for, yet surpasses it out of domain on GPQA for all three models, without any training, reward model, or labeled data.

</details>


### [246] [Low-Rank Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09694)
*Jonathan Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出低秩注意力残差（LR-AttnRes），通过将深度路由的key维度从完整模型宽度d降至低秩r（r≪d），在保持残差值完整维度的同时解耦路由与表征，并验证了两种高效实现方式的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力残差（Attention Residuals）将每个子层输出同时作为全维度的key和value，导致路由与表征耦合，且深度路由分数随隐藏宽度d缩放，效率低下。

Method: 提出LR-AttnRes：保持残差值全维度，使用r维低秩key进行路由。包含两种变体：(1) Projected LR-AttnRes：从现有输出投影中学习低秩key；(2) Sliced LR-AttnRes：直接取每个值的最后r维作为路由key，消除额外key投影路径。

Result: 在多种变体实验中，Projected LR-AttnRes取得最佳验证损失；Sliced LR-AttnRes在降低残差侧FLOPs的同时仍能提升性能。全面实验表明深度路由在远低于模型宽度的维度下依然有效。

Conclusion: 低秩路由可有效解耦路由与残差表征，大幅降低计算开销，为LLM深度路由提供了更高效的方案。

Abstract: Attention Residuals replace the fixed residual sum with depthwise attention over previous sub-layer outputs in large language models (LLMs), but use each output as both a full-dimensional key and value. This couples routing with representation and makes depth-routing scores scale with the hidden width $d$. We propose Low-Rank Attention Residuals (LR-AttnRes), which keep full-dimensional residual values while using $r$-dimensional keys, with $r \ll d$, for routing. Projected LR-AttnRes emits learned low-rank keys from existing output projections, decoupling routing from residual content and achieving the best validation loss among the variants tested. Sliced LR-AttnRes uses the last $r$ dimensions of each value as the routing key, removing the auxiliary key-projection path and reducing residual-side FLOPs while still improving performance. Comprehensive sweeps show that depthwise routing can be effective with far fewer dimensions than the model width. We release code and models to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [247] [HiFi-LLP: High-Fidelity, Low-Cost Latency Predictors with Confidence for Robust HW-NAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11746)
*Shambhavi Balamuthu Sampath,Behzad Shomali,Nael Fasfous,Moritz Thoma,Judeson Anthony Fernando,Lukas Frickenstein,Pierpaolo Mori,Manoj Rohit Vemparala,Alexander Frickenstein,Walter Stechele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HiFi-LLP，一种基于图注意力网络的高保真低代价延迟预测器，并设计混合NAS框架，通过置信度指标将低置信预测路由至硬件在环测量，在保持竞争性Pareto前沿的同时实现最高8.6倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件感知优化中，硬件在环延迟测量因顺序特性成为瓶颈；现有延迟预测器需大量样本且不准确会误导NAS过程。

Method: 提出HiFi-LLP：基于图注意力网络的延迟预测器，附带置信度指标。设计混合NAS框架：低置信预测路由至HIL测量。

Result: HiFi-LLP在10%精度边界上比先前预测器提升最多9个百分点，在LatBench六个设备上Spearman秩相关达0.996。混合NAS相比典型NAS实现最高8.6倍加速。

Conclusion: 高保真延迟预测器结合置信度路由机制能有效替代HIL反馈，大幅提升NAS效率。

Abstract: With deep neural networks (DNNs) increasingly deployed on edge devices, hardware (HW)-aware optimization techniques--such as HW-aware compression and HW-aware neural architecture search (HW-NAS)--have become essential.
